<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apnews.com/article/nvidia-megaspeed-china-singapore-huang-5403f152a601cf82ce939f1d24fffa0f" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang resigns</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋辞职</span></a><span class="dom">apnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession. The departure marks a leadership change at Nvidia.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。这意味着英伟达将发生领导层变动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>4043</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50467087' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3443</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50294702" data-id="50294702" data-tags="Entertainment" data-score="3103" data-comments="661" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702" data-time="1793302899">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart has died</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died. His work helped redefine leadership in science fiction and mentored generations of actors. The news prompts reflection on a prolific career that bridged stage, screen, and iconic franchises.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。其横跨舞台、电视和电影的职业生涯对科幻与表演行业产生深远影响。人们由此缅怀这位多产演员及其对文化的贡献。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>15.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>3103</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>661</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50378228" data-id="50378228" data-tags="Security" data-score="2978" data-comments="747" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228" data-time="1793881142">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sshhole.com/receipts/3bsd-passwd.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sshhole.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sshole</span><span class='zh'>sshole</span></a><span class="dom">sshhole.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016. The issue relates to how Unix historically represented accounts that should never permit login, including accounts marked with an asterisk in the password file. The behavior can therefore expose accounts that were intended to be inaccessible.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。问题与 Unix 处理本应禁止登录的账户的历史方式有关，包括在密码文件中用星号标记的账户。因此，原本应不可访问的账户可能被暴露。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>2978</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>747</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50341174" data-id="50341174" data-tags="AI" data-score="2928" data-comments="880" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341174" data-time="1793643688">
<a class="thumb" href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://rikiyat.github.io/images/blog/weight-space-programming.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='rikiyat.github.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen</span><span class='zh'>Astra 可读写权重以改进 Qwen</span></a><span class="dom">rikiyat.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly. GPT-6 Astra can infer what a network was trained to do from its raw weights and create small game-playing networks that outperform ones trained with PPO for 100 million steps. The capability could matter for model optimization and interpretability.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。GPT-6 Astra 可以仅凭原始权重判断网络的训练目标，还能从零编写击败经过 1 亿步 PPO 训练的小型游戏网络。这种能力可能对模型优化和可解释性具有重要意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://karpathy.bearblog.dev/hacker-tube/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HackerTube: YouTube built from videos recommended by HN</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube：由 HN 推荐视频构成的 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">karpathy.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007. The collection is available through hackertube.org. It turns years of community recommendations into a browsable video archive.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。该视频集合可通过 hackertube.org 浏览。它将多年社区推荐整理成了一个可探索的视频档案。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnbc.com/2026/11/23/nvidia-jensen-huang-ceo.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Reinstates Jensen Huang as CEO</span><span class='zh'>英伟达重新任命黄仁勋为 CEO</span></a><span class="dom">cnbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia has reinstated Jensen Huang as its CEO.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达重新任命黄仁勋为首席执行官。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://math216.wordpress.com/2026/11/20/hurried-announcement-alessandro-masullo-grh/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hurried Announcement: Alessandro Masullo GRH</span><span class='zh'>仓促公告：Alessandro Masullo 的广义黎曼猜想证明</span></a><span class="dom">math216.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alessandro Masullo has proposed a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis.</span><span class='zh'>Alessandro Masullo 提出了一份广义黎曼猜想的证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alessandro Masullo has proposed a proof of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis. Brian Conrad has reviewed it closely for months, provided extensive feedback, and considers the draft to be looking good so far. The authors had hoped to refine it at Stanford before announcing it, but decided to release the current draft so others could check and formalize it.</span><span class='zh'>Alessandro Masullo 提出了一份广义黎曼猜想的证明。Brian Conrad 已经对其进行了数月的仔细审阅，并提供了大量反馈，目前认为证明草稿看起来不错。原计划是在斯坦福进一步完善后再公布，但现在决定先发布当前版本，供其他人检查和形式化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/ylecun/status/2124550037912920090" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&quot;Dario is hyping the dangers so OSS models are illegal and he stays in control&quot;</span><span class='zh'>Dario 被指夸大风险，以推动 OSS 模型非法化并继续掌控局面</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post accuses Dario of consistently exaggerating AI dangers since GPT-2 in order to make open-source models illegal and retain control.</span><span class='zh'>该帖指责 Dario 自 GPT-2 以来一直夸大 AI 风险，目的是推动开源模型被宣布为非法并继续掌控局面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post accuses Dario of consistently exaggerating AI dangers since GPT-2 in order to make open-source models illegal and retain control. It also claims that he left OpenAI because he would not have control there. These are allegations made in the post, not independently established findings.</span><span class='zh'>该帖指责 Dario 自 GPT-2 以来一直夸大 AI 风险，目的是推动开源模型被宣布为非法并继续掌控局面。帖文还称，他离开 OpenAI 是因为无法在那里掌握控制权。这些内容是发帖者的指控，并非独立证实的事实。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackerbooks.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: HackerBooks – HackerTube, but for books mentioned on HN</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：HackerBooks——面向 HN 提及书籍的 HackerTube</span></a><span class="dom">hackerbooks.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today. Each book links back to the comments that mentioned it, and the dataset can be downloaded from the about page. The creator notes that the system counts mentions without distinguishing praise from criticism, so some entries may be missing or mismatched.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。每本书都链接回提及它的评论，数据集也可从关于页面下载。创建者说明，系统只统计提及次数，不区分赞扬和批评，因此部分书籍可能遗漏或匹配错误。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Binance-hacker-1029 – $5.6B stolen from Binance</span><span class='zh'>Binance 黑客案1029：从币安被盗5.6亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">arkm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches. On-chain tracing attempts to map the stolen funds as they move through wallets and mixers, showing how investigators pursue assets long after a hack. The lesson for builders is to strengthen custody security (multi-sig, cold storage) and incident response readiness.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。链上追踪尝试绘出赃款去向，显示即便多年后仍有追踪的可能。对系统设计者的启示是加强资产托管安全（多签、冷存储）与快速事件响应能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re releasing the weights of Mistral Large 4</span><span class='zh'>发布 Mistral Large 4 的权重</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally. This move reduces reliance on hosted APIs and accelerates experimentation, but also brings licensing, safety, and governance considerations to the fore in open-weight AI projects.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。此举降低对 API 的依赖，促进实验，但也引发许可、安全与治理方面的讨论。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-board-reinstates-jensen-huang-as-president-ceo-and-board-director" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang Reinstated as Nvidia CEO</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋复任英伟达首席执行官</span></a><span class="dom">nvidianews.nvidia.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA announced that Jensen Huang has been immediately reinstated as president, chief executive officer, and a member of the board.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达宣布，黄仁勋即日起复任总裁、首席执行官和董事会成员。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA announced that Jensen Huang has been immediately reinstated as president, chief executive officer, and a member of the board. The board voted unanimously after discussions with Huang and said it was satisfied with his candor and ability to lead the company. The board also received input from senior U.S. government officials and concluded that his reinstatement poses no national security risk.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达宣布，黄仁勋即日起复任总裁、首席执行官和董事会成员。董事会在与黄仁勋进行多次讨论后，一致投票支持复任，并表示认可他的坦诚以及领导公司的能力。董事会还听取了美国政府高层的意见，认定黄仁勋复任不会带来国家安全风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/news/us-news/faa-orders-nationwide-ground-stop-departing-flights-reasons-safety-rcna609247" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FAA issues national ground stop – lifted 4.5 hours later</span><span class='zh'>FAA 发布全国地面停飞令</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The FAA issued a national ground stop affecting departing flights.</span><span class='zh'>美国联邦航空管理局发布了影响出发航班的全国地面停飞令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The FAA issued a national ground stop affecting departing flights. The available records identify the action but do not provide reliable details about its duration, cause, or operational impact.</span><span class='zh'>美国联邦航空管理局发布了影响出发航班的全国地面停飞令。现有记录确认了这一措施，但未提供其持续时间、原因或运行影响的可靠细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/2130357/000162828026067168/anthropicpbc-sx1.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic S-1</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 S-1 招股书</span></a><span class="dom">sec.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO. The filing will reveal business metrics, product roadmap, and risk factors as the company scales its AI safety-focused offerings. Investors will weigh profitability trajectory, capital needs, and competitive positioning in a crowded AI market.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。文件将披露业务数据、产品路线图与风险因素，显示其在 AI 安全领域的扩张。投资者将评估盈利前景、资金需求以及在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中的定位。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/25/top_stories_10252026'><span class='en'>Oct 25</span><span class='zh'>10月25日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>1631</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1555</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50337608" data-id="50337608" data-tags="Security|AI" data-score="1609" data-comments="671" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50337608" data-time="1793631120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=chalmovsky.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>chalmovsky.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">19</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WTF Ubuntu?</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu，到底怎么回事？</span></a><span class="dom">chalmovsky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016. The source says Anthropic developed an exploit capable of gaining root access, while Debian issued a patch on October 16 and supported Ubuntu releases remained listed as vulnerable.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。资料称 Anthropic 已开发出可获取服务器 root 权限的漏洞利用程序；Debian 于 10 月 16 日发布了补丁，而受支持的 Ubuntu 版本仍被列为易受攻击。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1609</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50337608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>671</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50385272" data-id="50385272" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Code" data-score="1605" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272" data-time="1793917035">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">20</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches. The user says this happened without permission and was followed by a large increase in scam emails. They argue that the behavior should be removed rather than controlled by an opt-in or opt-out setting.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。该用户表示，未经允许发生这一行为后，自己收到的诈骗邮件大幅增加。用户认为，这种行为不应通过加入或退出选项控制，而应从系统中彻底移除。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1605</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>533</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50276998" data-id="50276998" data-tags="Privacy|Security|Web" data-score="1581" data-comments="306" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998" data-time="1793207609">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nullpt.rs/posts/fingerprinting-bioweapons/canvas-gallery.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nullpt.rs' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why is this fingerprinting script creating bioweapons?</span><span class='zh'>为什么这个指纹识别脚本在制造生物武器？</span></a><span class="dom">nullpt.rs</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai. They then considered crawling the web, intercepting fingerprinting-related browser API calls, and mapping what was found. The title connects this investigation with scripts that appear to be creating bioweapons, though the supplied material does not provide further details.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。随后，作者考虑抓取网页、拦截与指纹识别相关的浏览器 API 调用，并绘制其分布图。标题将这项调查与似乎正在制造生物武器的脚本联系起来，但现有内容未提供更多细节。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/28/top_stories_10282026'><span class='en'>Oct 28</span><span class='zh'>10月28日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1581</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>306</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50547374" data-id="50547374" data-tags="Security" data-score="1483" data-comments="637" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547374" data-time="1795015195">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wiz.io/blog/nginx-http2-rce-vulnerabilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Unauthenticated remote code execution in Nginx</span><span class='zh'>Nginx 中存在无需认证的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">wiz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nginx has been reported to contain unauthenticated remote code execution vulnerabilities.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Nginx 存在无需认证即可触发的远程代码执行漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nginx has been reported to contain unauthenticated remote code execution vulnerabilities. The supplied records identify two related CVE entries, CVE-2026-123633 and CVE-2026-123007.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Nginx 存在无需认证即可触发的远程代码执行漏洞。相关记录列出了两个 CVE 编号：CVE-2026-123633 和 CVE-2026-123007。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1483</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547374' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>637</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50581260" data-id="50581260" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1456" data-comments="293" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581260" data-time="1795205539">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/images/cover-social.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thinkingmachines.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/inkling-2-mini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inkling 2 Mini</span><span class='zh'>Inkling 2 Mini</span></a><span class="dom">thinkingmachines.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thinking Machines has released Inkling 2 Mini, an open-weights model small enough to run on a laptop.</span><span class='zh'>Thinking Machines 发布了 Inkling 2 Mini，这是一款小到可以在笔记本电脑上运行的开放权重模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thinking Machines has released Inkling 2 Mini, an open-weights model small enough to run on a laptop. It is a looped transformer that runs its middle layers three times per token, allowing it to reason more and use fewer tokens. The model reportedly leads open-weights models under 100 billion total parameters on DeepSWE v1.1 and Vals Index v2.2, while also running faster than similarly performing models.</span><span class='zh'>Thinking Machines 发布了 Inkling 2 Mini，这是一款小到可以在笔记本电脑上运行的开放权重模型。它采用循环 Transformer，每个 token 将中间层运行三次，从而进行更多推理并减少 token 使用量。据称，在总参数量低于 1000 亿的开放权重模型中，它在 DeepSWE v1.1 和 Vals Index v2.2 上处于领先，同时运行速度也快于性能相近的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><span class='en'>Nov 20</span><span class='zh'>11月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1456</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581260' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>293</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50450488" data-id="50450488" data-tags="Programming" data-score="1396" data-comments="187" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450488" data-time="1794380597">
<a class="thumb" href="https://discuss.python.org/t/in-memoriam-tim-peters-1952-2026/110028" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/flex002/uploads/python1/original/1X/f93ff97c4f381b5e8add5a0c163b4ded29f20ed7.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='discuss.python.org' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://discuss.python.org/t/in-memoriam-tim-peters-1952-2026/110028" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tim Peters has died</span><span class='zh'>Tim Peters 去世</span></a><span class="dom">discuss.python.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tim Peters has died. He was a figure associated with Python, but no further details are available here.</span><span class='zh'>Tim Peters 已去世。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tim Peters has died. He was a figure associated with Python, but no further details are available here.</span><span class='zh'>Tim Peters 已去世。他是与 Python 相关的重要人物，但现有信息未提供更多细节。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/nov/20/pill-pancreatic-cancer-breakthrough-daraxonrasib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pill to treat pancreatic cancer hailed as &#x27;biggest breakthrough in decades&#x27;</span><span class='zh'>治疗胰腺癌的药片被誉为“数十年来最大突破”</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In a trial of daraxonrasib, the cancer became undetectable in one in 12 patients with advanced pancreatic cancer.</span><span class='zh'>在 daraxonrasib 的一项试验中，12 名晚期胰腺癌患者中有 1 人的癌症变得无法检测到。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In a trial of daraxonrasib, the cancer became undetectable in one in 12 patients with advanced pancreatic cancer. Tumors stopped growing in nearly all of the 61 participants, whose cancer had spread to other parts of the body. The results are being hailed as a major advance in pancreatic cancer treatment.</span><span class='zh'>在 daraxonrasib 的一项试验中，12 名晚期胰腺癌患者中有 1 人的癌症变得无法检测到。61 名参与者的肿瘤几乎全部停止生长，这些患者的癌症此前已扩散到身体其他部位。这些结果被誉为胰腺癌治疗领域的重大进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.caranddriver.com/news/a74490092/toyota-nissan-honda-standardize-parts-lower-costs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda to standardize parts to lower costs</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田将统一零部件以降低成本</span></a><span class="dom">caranddriver.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda have agreed to standardize vehicle parts to reduce production costs as Japanese automakers compete with China’s growing automotive sector.</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田已同意统一汽车零部件标准，以降低生产成本，应对中国汽车产业的增长。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toyota, Nissan, and Honda have agreed to standardize vehicle parts to reduce production costs as Japanese automakers compete with China’s growing automotive sector. The initial effort will unify quality criteria for steel, protocols for plastics and resins, and color coding for wiring harnesses. The Japan Automobile Manufacturers Association plans to expand standardization to more components across the vehicle, focusing first on parts that are largely invisible to consumers and represent significant costs.</span><span class='zh'>丰田、日产和本田已同意统一汽车零部件标准，以降低生产成本，应对中国汽车产业的增长。首阶段将统一钢材质量标准、塑料和树脂的处理规范，以及线束的颜色编码。日本汽车工业协会计划未来将标准化范围扩大到更多整车零部件，首先选择消费者通常看不到且成本占比较高的部件。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">29</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/11/17/upshot/school-phone-bans-study-scores.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Evidence Shows School Phone Bans Are Gradually Improving Test Scores</span><span class='zh'>新证据显示，学校禁用手机正逐步提高考试成绩</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large national analysis comparing students’ scores before and after cellphone bans found modest academic improvements. Schools with strict policies requiring students to lock phones in pouches for the entire school day saw math and reading gains equivalent to roughly one or two additional weeks of schooling per year. The study was published by Harvard’s Center for Education Policy Research and is the first national study to track individual students’ growth in this way.</span><span class='zh'>一项比较学生禁用手机前后成绩的大型全国性分析发现，学业表现出现小幅改善。要求学生在整个上课日将手机锁入袋中的学校，数学和阅读成绩提升幅度相当于每年额外增加约一至两周的学习。该研究由哈佛教育政策研究中心发布，是首项以这种方式追踪个体学生成长的全国性研究。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1286</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50530163' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>642</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50364336" data-id="50364336" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="1271" data-comments="743" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336" data-time="1793793137">
<a class="thumb" href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=68544156&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C312%2C0%2C313' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='spectrum.ieee.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">30</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is forcing us to pay off 40 years of technical debt</span><span class='zh'>AI 正迫使我们偿还 40 年积累的技术债</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity. That acceleration is exposing painful technical debt in fragile legacy systems built up over roughly four decades. The pressure from AI is therefore forcing organizations to address weaknesses that older systems had left unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。这样的加速暴露了脆弱遗留系统中积累约 40 年的严重技术债。AI 带来的压力因此迫使组织处理旧系统长期遗留下来的弱点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1271</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>743</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50371246" data-id="50371246" data-tags="Security|Privacy|Politics" data-score="1249" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246" data-time="1793825118">
<a class="thumb" href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://layer3intel.com/eop_proxy_activity.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='layer3intel.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Residential proxies detected on White House network</span><span class='zh'>白宫网络被发现使用住宅代理</span></a><span class="dom">layer3intel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services. The network, AS2650, is registered to the Executive Office of the President, which hosts the White House network. Residential proxies route customers’ traffic through everyday devices so it appears to originate from those devices’ networks.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。该网络的 AS2650 注册于负责托管白宫网络的美国总统行政办公室。住宅代理会让客户的流量经由普通设备转发，使其看起来来自这些设备所在的网络。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1249</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>431</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50424581" data-id="50424581" data-tags="Education|Entertainment|Data" data-score="1246" data-comments="167" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50424581" data-time="1794226762">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight: The Best Paper Airplanes</span><span class='zh'>折叠飞行：最佳纸飞机</span></a><span class="dom">foldableflight.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. One World Record prototype is reported to fly more than 300 feet. The Unite model holds a world record for time aloft at 31.2 seconds.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。据称，一款世界纪录原型机可以飞行超过 300 英尺。Unite 机型则以 31.2 秒的滞空时间保持世界纪录。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge released a Lean library containing a proof of the Riemann Hypothesis on November 20, 2026.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 20 日，Levent Alpöge 发布了一个包含黎曼猜想证明的 Lean 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Levent Alpöge released a Lean library containing a proof of the Riemann Hypothesis on November 20, 2026. The library comprises 17 million lines of code. The Lean and Mathlib communities issued a joint statement endorsing the verification, with support from the Lean FRO and the Mathlib Initiative.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年 11 月 20 日，Levent Alpöge 发布了一个包含黎曼猜想证明的 Lean 库。该库包含 1700 万行代码。Lean 和 Mathlib 社区随后发表联合声明，Lean FRO 与 Mathlib Initiative 也表示支持这项验证工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calif.io/java4shell/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Java4Shell: RCE in OpenJDK when processing Base64</span><span class='zh'>Java4Shell：处理 Base64 时 OpenJDK 中的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">calif.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Calif disclosed Java4Shell, tracked as CVE-2026-114938, a memory-corruption vulnerability in OpenJDK&#x27;s Base64 decoder. OpenJDK is described as the most popular Java runtime. The vulnerability has a CVSS 4.0 score of 9.2 out of 10, rated Critical. Its severity could make affected OpenJDK deployments a high-priority patching target.</span><span class='zh'>Calif 披露了 Java4Shell（编号 CVE-2026-114938），这是 OpenJDK Base64 解码器中的内存破坏漏洞。OpenJDK 被称为最流行的 Java 运行时。该漏洞在 CVSS 4.0 中获得 9.2 分（满分 10 分），评级为“严重”。这一评级意味着受影响的 OpenJDK 部署可能需要优先修补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/apple.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts the compromised eMudhra certificates</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust. This highlights how supply chain security depends on up-to-date trust stores, and warrants prompt certificate revocation and rotation.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。这凸显供应链安全对最新信任存储的依赖，应尽快撤销并轮换相关证书。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades. The findings raise questions about civil liberties, oversight, and the balance between border enforcement and political dissent.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。相关证据推动对公民自由、监督机制以及边境执法与政治异议之间平衡的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arm.jonpalmisc.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Per Arm&#x27;s request, this site will be discontinued on 4 December 2026 :(</span><span class='zh'>应 Arm 要求，该网站将于 2026 年 12 月 4 日停止运营</span></a><span class="dom">arm.jonpalmisc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site provides reference documentation for the AArch64 instruction set and system registers in the Armv8-A and Armv9-A architectures.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供 Armv8-A 和 Armv9-A 架构的 AArch64 指令集及系统寄存器参考文档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site provides reference documentation for the AArch64 instruction set and system registers in the Armv8-A and Armv9-A architectures. Its operator says Arm&#x27;s lawyers demanded that it be taken down or face legal action, so it will be discontinued on December 4, 2026. The operator is investigating ways to keep existing bookmarks and links working.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供 Armv8-A 和 Armv9-A 架构的 AArch64 指令集及系统寄存器参考文档。运营者表示，Arm 的律师要求其关闭，否则将面临法律行动，因此网站将于 2026 年 12 月 4 日停止运营。运营者正在寻找保留现有书签和链接可用的方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://1password.com/blog/update-on-nov-2026-security-incident" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1Password Security Incident</span><span class='zh'>1Password 安全事件</span></a><span class="dom">1password.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>1Password identified an incident in which an attacker accessed an employee’s corporate GitHub account on November 19, 2026.</span><span class='zh'>1Password 发现了一起安全事件：攻击者于 2026 年 11 月 19 日访问了一名员工的企业 GitHub 账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>1Password identified an incident in which an attacker accessed an employee’s corporate GitHub account on November 19, 2026. The attacker used it to access source code, push malicious code to backend services, and download customer-related administrative data, including names, email addresses, and non-secret metadata; records for a subset of users were also downloaded. Customer vault contents were not accessed and remain secure.</span><span class='zh'>1Password 发现了一起安全事件：攻击者于 2026 年 11 月 19 日访问了一名员工的企业 GitHub 账户。攻击者利用该账户访问源代码、向后端服务推送恶意代码，并下载包含客户姓名、电子邮件地址及非机密账户元数据的管理数据；部分用户的账户记录也被下载。客户保险库内容未被访问，仍然安全。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/building-scientific-understanding-with-academic-communities/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries in mathematics, computer science, and physics</span><span class='zh'>宣布数学、计算机科学与物理学三项新发现</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science. The announcement hints at novel methods and insights that could influence both theory and practice across disciplines.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。公告暗示可能的新方法与洞见，影响理论到实践的研究路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smart home devices should work without the internet</span><span class='zh'>智能家居应当无需互联网也能运行</span></a><span class="dom">docodethatmatters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server. As a result, the home continues operating when the internet goes down. The post presents the other automation rules and two related talks that grew out of this principle.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。因此，即使互联网中断，房屋仍能继续运行。文章还整理了由这一原则延伸出的其他自动化规则和两场相关演讲。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla to wind down Cybertruck production</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉将逐步停止生产 Cybertruck</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027. He said the move will make room for higher production of the newly launched Cybercab, central to Tesla&#x27;s robotaxi plans. Cybertruck sales have remained weak, and it has sold in only a limited number of markets. It remains unclear whether production will move elsewhere or end entirely.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。他称，此举将为提高新款 Cybercab 的产量腾出空间，而 Cybercab 是特斯拉机器人出租车计划的核心车型。Cybertruck 销量长期疲软，目前仅在少数市场销售。该车型是转移到其他工厂生产还是彻底停产，目前尚不清楚。</span></p>

<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1130</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562754' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2296</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50507303" data-id="50507303" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1094" data-comments="595" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50507303" data-time="1794754018">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/img/en/ai-code-open-source.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jakub.jankiewicz.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">46</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jakub.jankiewicz.org/blog/ai-code-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All AI-generated code should be Open Source</span><span class='zh'>所有 AI 生成的代码都应开源</span></a><span class="dom">jakub.jankiewicz.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that all code generated with AI assistance should be released as open source or under a CC0 license. The argument is based on AI models being trained on large amounts of publicly available open-source code, as well as Stack Overflow and related sites whose Creative Commons licenses generally require attribution. The author says turning that shared knowledge into proprietary code resembles license laundering.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张，所有借助 AI 生成的代码都应以开源许可证或 CC0 许可发布。其理由是，AI 模型训练使用了大量公开的开源代码，以及通常要求署名的 Stack Overflow 和相关网站内容。作者认为，将这些共享知识转化为专有闭源代码近似于“许可证洗白”。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>10.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1094</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50507303' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>595</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50606192" data-id="50606192" data-tags="Web" data-score="1088" data-comments="186" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50606192" data-time="1795396431">
<a class="thumb" href="https://spelunkr.tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://spelunkr.tv/og-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='spelunkr.tv' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spelunkr.tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Free alternative TV network built from the Internet Archive&#x27;s forgotten videos</span><span class='zh'>用互联网档案馆遗忘的视频打造免费替代电视网</span></a><span class="dom">spelunkr.tv</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project provides synchronized 24/7 channels built from forgotten videos in the Internet Archive.</span><span class='zh'>该项目利用互联网档案馆中被遗忘的视频，提供全天候同步播放的频道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project provides synchronized 24/7 channels built from forgotten videos in the Internet Archive. It is designed to resemble cable television, but with a deliberately stranger selection of programming.</span><span class='zh'>该项目利用互联网档案馆中被遗忘的视频，提供全天候同步播放的频道。它模仿有线电视的形式，但节目选择更加古怪。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>1088</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50606192' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>186</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50399933" data-id="50399933" data-tags="Programming" data-score="1077" data-comments="194" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50399933" data-time="1794007852">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mitchellh.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mitchellh.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">48</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libghostty 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Libghostty 1.0</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core. Both the library and its C ABI are now stable. The C API has grown from nonexistent to a complete terminal emulator used in production by millions of people across hundreds of free and commercial products.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。该库本身及其 C ABI 现已稳定。其 C API 已从无到有发展为完整的终端模拟器，目前已在数百款免费和商业产品中投入生产，服务数百万人。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-us-government-is-hiding-a-secret-court-ruling-about-how-it-spies-on-americans/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Government is hiding a secret court ruling about how it spies on Americans</span><span class='zh'>美国政府隐瞒关于监控美国人的秘密法院裁决</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret. The ruling highlights ongoing friction between security powers and civil liberties, underscoring the demand for greater transparency and robust oversight.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。该裁决暴露出国家安全权力与公民自由之间的矛盾，也凸显对更大透明度和强有力监督的诉求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techpowerup.com/354814/nvidia-allegedly-engineered-scarcity-by-funding-ai-startups-to-buy-its-gpus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia allegedly engineered scarcity by funding AI startups to buy its GPUs</span><span class='zh'>英伟达被指通过资助 AI 初创公司购买 GPU，人为制造供应紧张</span></a><span class="dom">techpowerup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new report, citing The Wall Street Journal, alleges that NVIDIA increased the GPU and memory shortage by investing in AI startups that then bought NVIDIA GPUs and built data centers. The allegation emerged after Jensen Huang’s departure as NVIDIA’s CEO, according to the supplied report. The claim concerns whether the company’s financing activity helped inflate demand for its own hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一份援引《华尔街日报》的新报告称，英伟达通过投资 AI 初创公司，促使这些公司购买英伟达 GPU 并建设数据中心，从而加剧了 GPU 和内存短缺。报告称，这一指控是在 Jensen Huang 离任英伟达 CEO 后出现的。争议焦点在于，英伟达的融资活动是否帮助推高了对其自有硬件的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/genie-4-a-new-generation-of-world-simulation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Genie 4</span><span class='zh'>Genie 4：世界仿真的新一代</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning. It aims to accelerate progress in robotics, game AI, and simulation-to-real transfer by providing richer, more controllable environments.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://honnibal.dev/blog/astra-read-write-misleading" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The &#x27;Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen&#x27; post is misleading</span><span class='zh'>“Astra 能读写权重来改进 Qwen”的说法具有误导性</span></a><span class="dom">honnibal.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques. They compare the presentation to a series of stage-magic tricks that appear to test competing explanations while relying on different mechanisms.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。作者将这种呈现方式比作一连串舞台魔术：表面上是在检验相互竞争的解释，实际却依赖不同机制。文章分析了原始说法为何可能让人误解这些实验究竟证明了什么。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1030</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50351111' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>207</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50452455" data-id="50452455" data-tags="AI|Science|Space|Politics" data-score="1027" data-comments="390" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455" data-time="1794398722">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site/forest-vs-lidar.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pietromiotti.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">53</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My AI agents found an unmapped Maya site?</span><span class='zh'>我的 AI 代理发现了一个未标记的玛雅遗址？</span></a><span class="dom">pietromiotti.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico. The site had already been mapped by archaeologists and described in Estrada-Belli et al. 2025, but it was not yet listed in Mexico’s official INAH register. The agents therefore rediscovered the site rather than discovering it, and the process took less than 72 hours to reach an official response from the Mexican government.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。考古学家其实已经绘制过该遗址的地图，并在 Estrada-Belli 等人 2025 年的论文中记录了它，只是它尚未列入墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）的官方名录。因此，代理完成的是“重新发现”而非发现；从提出想法到收到墨西哥政府的正式回复耗时不到 72 小时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1027</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>390</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50597612" data-id="50597612" data-tags="Energy|Science|AI" data-score="1027" data-comments="1005" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50597612" data-time="1795337854">
<a class="thumb" href="https://betterdatacenterproject.com/2026/11/16/how-ai-is-powered-a-breakdown-of-byop-and-grid-power/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i0.wp.com/betterdatacenterproject.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/anthropic-total-co2.png?fit=1200%2C1065&amp;ssl=1' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='betterdatacenterproject.com' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">54</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://betterdatacenterproject.com/2026/11/16/how-ai-is-powered-a-breakdown-of-byop-and-grid-power/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>87% of the Anthropic power will come from gas and 11% from coal in 2028</span><span class='zh'>2028 年 Anthropic 的电力将有 87% 来自天然气、11% 来自煤炭</span></a><span class="dom">betterdatacenterproject.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A case study estimates that Anthropic could emit 32 million metric tons of carbon dioxide in 2028.</span><span class='zh'>一项案例研究估计，Anthropic 在 2028 年可能排放 3,200 万吨二氧化碳。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A case study estimates that Anthropic could emit 32 million metric tons of carbon dioxide in 2028. It examines the company’s total power use and the role of dedicated power generation for AI data centers, noting that planned bring-your-own-power gas capacity is far more common than renewable generation. The study also identifies another 200 GW of new fossil-fuel power plants being built on the grid, partly to meet data-center demand.</span><span class='zh'>一项案例研究估计，Anthropic 在 2028 年可能排放 3,200 万吨二氧化碳。研究考察了该公司的总用电量，以及 AI 数据中心专用发电的作用，并指出已规划的“自带电力”项目中，天然气发电远多于可再生能源。研究还指出，为满足包括数据中心在内的增长需求，电网上另有 200 吉瓦新的化石燃料发电厂正在建设。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1027</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50597612' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1005</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50637220" data-id="50637220" data-tags="Science|Code" data-score="1025" data-comments="580" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50637220" data-time="1795569566">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A preprint claims that the matrix multiplication exponent is nine fourths.</span><span class='zh'>一篇预印本宣称，矩阵乘法指数为九分之四。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A preprint claims that the matrix multiplication exponent is nine fourths.</span><span class='zh'>一篇预印本宣称，矩阵乘法指数为九分之四。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fzakaria.com/2026/11/23/2035-came-early" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>2035 came early</span><span class='zh'>2035 年提前到来了</span></a><span class="dom">fzakaria.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>trynix.dev can now boot GUI programs, including Solitaire, GL gears, xeyes, a voice application, weather data fetched through curl, and Firefox.</span><span class='zh'>trynix.dev 现在已经能够启动图形界面程序，包括纸牌、GL gears、xeyes、语音应用、通过 curl 获取的天气信息以及 Firefox。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>trynix.dev can now boot GUI programs, including Solitaire, GL gears, xeyes, a voice application, weather data fetched through curl, and Firefox. The project is presented in the context of the long-running idea that applications written in JavaScript will eventually be written in JavaScript. Its expanding ability to run graphical software illustrates how far JavaScript-based environments have progressed.</span><span class='zh'>trynix.dev 现在已经能够启动图形界面程序，包括纸牌、GL gears、xeyes、语音应用、通过 curl 获取的天气信息以及 Firefox。该项目被置于“凡是能用 JavaScript 编写的应用，最终都会用 JavaScript 编写”这一长期观点的背景下。它对图形软件支持范围的扩大，展示了基于 JavaScript 的环境已经发展到何种程度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-neon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Neon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Neon</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has introduced Gemini 4 Neon, a model aimed at complex coding, knowledge work, and scientific workflows. The company says it offers frontier-level intelligence with fast end-to-end task completion at a lower cost. The model is presented by Google DeepMind’s senior vice president and chief AI architect.</span><span class='zh'>Google 推出了 Gemini 4 Neon，面向复杂编程、知识工作和科学工作流。Google 表示，该模型以更低成本提供前沿级智能，并能快速完成端到端任务。该模型由 Google DeepMind 高级副总裁兼首席 AI 架构师发布。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic IPO</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 筹备首次公开募股</span></a><span class="dom">ipo.anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information. The company is organized as a public benefit corporation focused on securing AI’s benefits and mitigating its risks. It says AI could accelerate scientific innovation, increase economic growth, help cure major diseases, and address long-standing problems if managed well.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。该公司采用公益公司架构，致力于发挥人工智能的益处并降低其风险。Anthropic 表示，如果得到妥善管理，人工智能可能加速科学创新、提高经济增长率、推动重大疾病治疗，并解决长期未能解决的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-4-carbon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Carbon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Carbon：谷歌推出的 Gemini 系列新一代模型</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Carbon is Google&#x27;s latest in the Gemini family, focusing on efficiency and safety with improved inference and reliability for enterprise apps.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Carbon 作为谷歌 Gemini 系列的新一代模型，强调在企业应用中的推理效率与安全性提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Carbon is Google&#x27;s latest in the Gemini family, focusing on efficiency and safety with improved inference and reliability for enterprise apps. It demonstrates ongoing progress in AI model scaling, with Carbon as a more efficient variant for dense workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Carbon 作为谷歌 Gemini 系列的新一代模型，强调在企业应用中的推理效率与安全性提升。该系列继续推动大模型规模化，Carbon 版本在高密度任务中更具效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.crissyfield.de/research/lovable-en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lovable apps expose passport details and more than 200K private messages</span><span class='zh'>Lovable 应用暴露护照信息和超过 20 万条私人消息</span></a><span class="dom">crissyfield.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 159,041 Lovable projects found many cases in which personal and confidential information was accessible without logging in, including passport details and more than 200,000 private messages. Lovable is a Stockholm-based Swedish AI startup founded in 2023 by Anton Osika and Fabian Hedin, whose platform generates web applications from plain-language descriptions. The findings show that building applications with AI does not remove the need for careful handling of sensitive data.</span><span class='zh'>对 159,041 个 Lovable 项目的分析发现，许多项目无需登录即可访问个人和机密信息，其中包括护照详情以及超过 20 万条私人消息。Lovable 是一家总部位于斯德哥尔摩、由 Anton Osika 和 Fabian Hedin 于 2023 年创立的瑞典 AI 初创公司，其平台可根据自然语言描述生成 Web 应用。这些发现表明，使用 AI 构建应用并不能免除对敏感数据进行谨慎处理的要求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.seattletimes.com/business/microsoft/microsoft-offers-mass-buyouts-for-roughly-a-quarter-of-its-workforce/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft offers mass buyouts for roughly a quarter of its workforce</span><span class='zh'>微软为约四分之一员工提供大规模自愿离职计划</span></a><span class="dom">seattletimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs. The move fits into broader tech-sector efficiency drives as AI investments continue and macro pressures persist. It could reshape compensation bands, talent flow, and project prioritization across divisions.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。此举与科技行业的整体效率提升趋势相吻合，AI 投资与宏观压力并行推动成本优化。人力结构、人才流向和项目优先级可能因此调整。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/25/top_stories_10252026'><span class='en'>Oct 25</span><span class='zh'>10月25日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>967</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245524' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>949</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50517488" data-id="50517488" data-tags="Business" data-score="954" data-comments="551" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50517488" data-time="1794836347">
<a class="thumb" href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='http://groundworkcollaborative.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/GWC_CL_Cover_110526-1-791x1024.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='groundworkcollaborative.org' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">64</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="http://groundworkcollaborative.org/news/amazons-decoy-deals-groundwork-investigation-reveals-nearly-1-in-3-black-friday-and-cyber-monday-deals-advertised-by-amazon-offered-no-savings-to-shoppers-nearly-1-in-10-w/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Many of Amazon’s Black Friday and Cyber Monday “deals” offered no savings</span><span class='zh'>亚马逊许多黑色星期五和网络星期一“优惠”并没有省钱</span></a><span class="dom">groundworkcollaborative.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigation found that many Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals advertised by Amazon did not provide actual savings. The deals were presented as discounts even though shoppers often paid no less than usual.</span><span class='zh'>一项调查发现，亚马逊宣传的许多黑色星期五和网络星期一优惠实际上并没有带来节省。这些商品虽然以折扣形式展示，消费者实际支付的价格却往往并未降低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.nns.ee/2026/11/02/renault-hack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jailbreaking my car&#x27;s navigation system just so I can change the time</span><span class='zh'>为了改时间，我破解了汽车的导航系统</span></a><span class="dom">blog.nns.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes modifying the navigation system of a 2014 Renault Latitude to change the dashboard clock.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了如何修改一辆 2014 年雷诺 Latitude 的导航系统，以调整仪表盘时钟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes modifying the navigation system of a 2014 Renault Latitude to change the dashboard clock. The car apparently required a navigation-map SD card for access to the time-setting function, even though the card had been discarded because its maps were outdated. Without it, the display showed a prominent “No maps found!” message.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了如何修改一辆 2014 年雷诺 Latitude 的导航系统，以调整仪表盘时钟。该车似乎需要导航地图 SD 卡才能访问时间设置功能，但由于地图过时，这张卡此前已被丢弃。没有 SD 卡时，显示屏会显示醒目的“No maps found!”提示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neil.fraser.name/news/2026/11/10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.name Grandfathered</span><span class='zh'>.name 域名获得保留</span></a><span class="dom">neil.fraser.name</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign. New registrations for third-level .name domains will close, but all existing domains and redirects will remain indefinitely. The reversal followed efforts by domain owners, lawyers, and supporters to challenge the plan through ICANN.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。虽然 .name 三级域名将停止新注册，但现有域名和邮件转发服务将无限期保留。这一转变源于域名所有者、律师和支持者通过 ICANN 发起的共同争取。</span></p>
