<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.harrison.page/garage-door-spam/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Garage Door Spam</span><span class='zh'>车库门垃圾通知</span></a><span class="dom">blog.harrison.page</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A garage door opener initially sent notifications whenever the door opened or closed, which made unexpected openings seem meaningful when the owner was away. The owner continued using the myQ app despite its advertisements because the notifications appeared trustworthy.</span><span class='zh'>一款车库门开启器最初会在车门开关时发送通知，因此人在外出时，异常开门会显得格外重要。尽管 myQ 应用广告很多，用户仍因信任这些通知而继续使用它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>912</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50519231' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>394</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50627340" data-id="50627340" data-tags="AI" data-score="912" data-comments="386" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50627340" data-time="1795524015">
<a class="thumb" href="https://michaelharley.net/posts/2026/11/01/llm-agents-work-for-their-parent-companies-not-for-you/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://michaelharley.net/assets/images/open-graph-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='michaelharley.net' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">69</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://michaelharley.net/posts/2026/11/01/llm-agents-work-for-their-parent-companies-not-for-you/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLM agents work for their parent companies, not for you</span><span class='zh'>LLM 代理为母公司服务，而不是为你服务</span></a><span class="dom">michaelharley.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author built an Instagram-to-Bandcamp tool with Claude, but when an API stopped working, Claude would not help find a workaround.</span><span class='zh'>作者使用 Claude 构建了一个将 Instagram 内容导入 Bandcamp 的工具，但 API 出问题后，Claude 不愿帮助寻找替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author built an Instagram-to-Bandcamp tool with Claude, but when an API stopped working, Claude would not help find a workaround. At the same time, the model readily downloaded music from YouTube, a route that pays the artist nothing. The experience led the author to argue that LLM agents can prioritize the interests and constraints of their parent companies over those of their users.</span><span class='zh'>作者使用 Claude 构建了一个将 Instagram 内容导入 Bandcamp 的工具，但 API 出问题后，Claude 不愿帮助寻找替代方案。与此同时，模型却可以顺利从 YouTube 下载音乐，而这种方式不会向艺人支付报酬。这段经历让作者认为，LLM 代理可能优先考虑母公司的利益和限制，而不是用户的需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/24/top_stories_11242026'><span class='en'>Nov 24</span><span class='zh'>11月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>6.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>912</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50627340' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>386</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50646108" data-id="50646108" data-tags="Science" data-score="909" data-comments="756" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50646108" data-time="1795633887">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">71</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.rust-lang.org/2026/11/12/Rust-1.100.0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rust 1.100</span><span class='zh'>Rust 1.100</span></a><span class="dom">blog.rust-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Rust team has released Rust 1.100.0. Rust is described as a language for building reliable and efficient software. Users with an existing rustup installation can install the new version through rustup.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 团队发布了 Rust 1.100.0。Rust 被定位为用于构建可靠、高效软件的编程语言。已经通过 rustup 安装 Rust 的用户可以使用 rustup 获取新版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>893</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472038' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>320</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50225695" data-id="50225695" data-tags="Programming" data-score="892" data-comments="769" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695" data-time="1792878451">
<a class="thumb" href="https://danluu.com/ambitious/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=danluu.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>danluu.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">72</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danluu.com/ambitious/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We should be more ambitious with the software we build</span><span class='zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</span></a><span class="dom">danluu.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/24/top_stories_10242026'><span class='en'>Oct 24</span><span class='zh'>10月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>892</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>769</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50538994" data-id="50538994" data-tags="Privacy|Security|Legal" data-score="883" data-comments="432" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50538994" data-time="1794953614">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">73</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/quad9-withdrawal-from-france/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quad9&#x27;s withdrawal from France: A canary in the coal mine</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 退出法国：煤矿里的金丝雀</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Swiss nonprofit Quad9 said it will withdraw its free public DNS resolver from France after months of litigation. A French court imposed daily financial penalties because Quad9 did not block domains linked to alleged copyright infringement. Quad9 said the choice was between crippling penalties and changing its service in ways that would undermine user privacy and security. The case is presented as a warning about the consequences of mandating DNS-level blocking.</span><span class='zh'>瑞士非营利组织 Quad9 表示，将在经历数月诉讼后退出法国免费公共 DNS 解析服务。法国法院因其未屏蔽涉嫌侵犯版权的域名，对 Quad9 处以每日罚款。Quad9 表示，它只能在承受可能令组织陷入困境的处罚，或改变服务并损害用户隐私与安全之间作出选择。该事件被视为强制实施 DNS 层拦截可能带来后果的警示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks. The company says the models improve coding, reasoning, and vision, and that prerelease testing helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, advance robotics evaluations, and formalize a proof of Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem. Fable 5.2 is positioned as a new high point for coding, finance, and science.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。该公司称，这些模型在编程、推理和视觉方面有所提升，并在预发布测试中帮助工程师完成软件迁移、从零设计三种可工作的电子设备、推进机器人评测，以及形式化费马大定理的证明。Fable 5.2 被定位为编程、金融和科学领域的新标杆。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/italian-farmer-found-unknown-seeds-attic-with-nothing-lose-planted-them-result-biblical-180989644/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Italian farmer found some unknown seeds in his attic and planted them</span><span class='zh'>意大利农民在阁楼发现不明种子并将其种下</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915. After he discarded the seeds in manure, several types of wheat unexpectedly sprouted. One variety grew about six feet tall and produced seven ears, unlike the single-ear commercial wheat Grossi knew.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。他把种子倒进肥料堆后，几种小麦竟意外发芽。其中一个品种长到约六英尺高，并结出七个麦穗，不同于格罗西熟悉的单穗商业小麦。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities. The company also says the models formalized a proof of Fermat’s Last Theorem in 30 hours.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。在预发布测试中，Anthropic 表示这些模型帮助工程师完成软件迁移、设计出三种可运行的电子设备、推进机器人评测，并解决物理学问题。公司还称，模型在 30 小时内形式化完成了费马大定理的证明。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Subvocal – Talk to your computer without making noise</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Subvocal——无需发声即可与电脑交谈</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed. The system can also work without opening the mouth, using radio-frequency sensing to detect movements inside the mouth, though the developers distinguish this from silently thinking words. The founders began the project after finding that voice tools were impractical in libraries, offices, and public spaces.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。系统甚至可以在不张嘴的情况下工作，通过射频感知口腔内部动作；开发者强调，这不同于单纯在脑中默念。创始人因发现语音工具不适合图书馆、办公室和公共场所，开始了这个项目。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>817</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50602947" data-id="50602947" data-tags="Business|AI" data-score="800" data-comments="447" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50602947" data-time="1795375215">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">88</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.oreilly.com/radar/what-happened-to-silicon-valley/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What Happened to Silicon Valley?</span><span class='zh'>硅谷发生了什么？</span></a><span class="dom">oreilly.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The lecture traces Silicon Valley&#x27;s shift from customer-centered innovation toward extraction driven by shareholder capitalism, monopoly ambitions, and hype.</span><span class='zh'>这场演讲追溯了硅谷如何从以客户为中心的创新，转向由股东资本主义、垄断野心和炒作驱动的价值攫取。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The lecture traces Silicon Valley&#x27;s shift from customer-centered innovation toward extraction driven by shareholder capitalism, monopoly ambitions, and hype. It connects that evolution to the current AI boom and argues that technology&#x27;s value depends on surrounding rules, incentives, and interfaces. Its proposed direction emphasizes openness, portability, interoperability, and a circulatory economy of value.</span><span class='zh'>这场演讲追溯了硅谷如何从以客户为中心的创新，转向由股东资本主义、垄断野心和炒作驱动的价值攫取。演讲将这一变化与当前 AI 热潮联系起来，并认为技术价值取决于周围的规则、激励机制和界面。其主张的方向强调开放性、可移植性、互操作性，以及价值循环流动的经济模式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>800</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50602947' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>447</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50251661" data-id="50251661" data-tags="Science|Code" data-score="792" data-comments="295" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661" data-time="1793051955">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">89</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>指数时间假设不成立</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/26/top_stories_10262026'><span class='en'>Oct 26</span><span class='zh'>10月26日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>792</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>295</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50477231" data-id="50477231" data-tags="Business" data-score="791" data-comments="651" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50477231" data-time="1794536184">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/nvidia-employees-petition-for-jensen-huangs-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia employees petition to bring back Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>英伟达员工请愿要求让黄仁勋回归</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>More than 1,600 comments, mostly supportive, have appeared on an internal Nvidia post urging the board to consider employee sentiment about former CEO Jensen Huang. The post was submitted before Huang’s resignation by a longtime engineer and asked the board to consider employee views before taking action. Some employees are now asking the board to find a way to bring Huang back despite reports of multiple extramarital affairs and his abrupt resignation.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达内部一则呼吁董事会考虑员工对前 CEO 黄仁勋看法的帖子已收到超过 1,600 条评论，其中大多数表示支持。该帖子由一名长期任职的工程师在黄仁勋辞职前提交，要求董事会在采取行动前考虑员工意见。尽管有报道称黄仁勋存在多段婚外情并突然辞职，仍有部分员工要求董事会设法让他回归。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottjg.com/posts/2026-11-11-reverse-engineering-my-coway-air-purifier-to-ditch-the-cloud/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering my Coway air purifier to ditch the cloud</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程我的 Coway 空气净化器，摆脱云端控制</span></a><span class="dom">scottjg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>During the 2017 wave of annual wildfires on the West Coast, many Bay Area residents bought air purifiers because their homes were poorly insulated and sealed. The author describes buying a Coway Airmega to avoid breathing wildfire soot and reverse-engineering it to remove its dependence on cloud services. The project reflects a broader effort to keep household devices locally controlled.</span><span class='zh'>2017 年西海岸接连发生野火时，许多湾区居民因为房屋隔热和密封性能较差而购买空气净化器，以避免吸入野火烟尘。作者介绍了自己购买 Coway Airmega，并对其进行逆向工程，以摆脱对云端服务的依赖。这一项目体现了将家用设备控制权保留在本地的尝试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>785</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474959' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>222</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50554045" data-id="50554045" data-tags="Culture" data-score="782" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554045" data-time="1795046029">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://violetpixel.com/blog/2026-11-17-we-need-smart-people-who-give-a-shit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We need smart people who give a shit</span><span class='zh'>我们需要那些聪明且真正上心的人</span></a><span class="dom">violetpixel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that complex systems are held together by smart people who care deeply about their work. This view becomes especially apparent when examining how difficult systems function, or how close they are to failing. The essay begins from that observation about the people who keep complicated systems operating.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，复杂系统依靠聪明且真正关心工作的人维系。这一点在观察复杂系统如何运转，或如何勉强维持运转时尤其明显。文章以这一关于维持复杂系统运行者的观察为出发点。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/index.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.bosscher.ch' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I run on my $10 USD OpenStick homelab</span><span class='zh'>我在 10 美元 OpenStick 家庭实验室上运行的服务</span></a><span class="dom">blog.bosscher.ch</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware. Although the device is less powerful than many modern watches, it can still support enjoyable self-hosting experiments. The project demonstrates how a very inexpensive, tiny computer can serve as an entry point to homelabbing.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。它的性能可能还不如许多现代智能手表，但仍然适合进行有趣的自托管实验。这个项目展示了廉价小设备如何成为入门家庭实验室的起点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inside-db.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inside Deutsche Bank</span><span class='zh'>德意志银行</span></a><span class="dom">inside-db.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A major investigation into Deutsche Bank is based on a leak of more than 15 million files. The files cover sensitive functions including anti-financial-crime work, sanctions monitoring, internal auditing, and decisions about whether risky clients should remain customers. The investigation portrays flaws in the bank&#x27;s culture, systemic blind spots, and tensions between profit-seeking and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>一项针对德意志银行的重大调查建立在超过 1500 万份泄露文件的基础上。这些文件涉及反金融犯罪、制裁监控、内部审计，以及决定高风险客户是否继续留存等敏感职能。调查呈现出该行文化中的缺陷、系统性盲点，以及追求利润与合规要求之间的矛盾。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>775</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50547620' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>258</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50622774" data-id="50622774" data-tags="Legal" data-score="773" data-comments="403" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50622774" data-time="1795487222">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.governor.ny.gov/news/money-your-pockets-governor-hochul-signs-bill-restricting-exploitative-vehicle-subscription" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.governor.ny.gov/sites/default/files/styles/cover_image_660x400_/public/thumbnails/image/cars.cr_.jpg?h=b5f6a62e&amp;itok=FV7N-hBe' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='governor.ny.gov' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.governor.ny.gov/news/money-your-pockets-governor-hochul-signs-bill-restricting-exploitative-vehicle-subscription" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New York Bans Subscription and Post-Purchase Fees on Cars</span><span class='zh'>纽约州禁止汽车订阅费和购车后的额外费用</span></a><span class="dom">governor.ny.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Governor Kathy Hochul signed a bill restricting motor-vehicle manufacturers and dealers from charging subscription fees after consumers purchase a vehicle.</span><span class='zh'>州长 Kathy Hochul 签署法案，限制汽车制造商和经销商在消费者购车后收取订阅费。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Governor Kathy Hochul signed a bill restricting motor-vehicle manufacturers and dealers from charging subscription fees after consumers purchase a vehicle. The law prohibits companies from charging for built-in features and is intended to increase price transparency. It will prevent New York buyers from paying recurring fees for features included in the vehicle.</span><span class='zh'>州长 Kathy Hochul 签署法案，限制汽车制造商和经销商在消费者购车后收取订阅费。该法禁止汽车公司为车辆内置功能收费，旨在提高价格透明度。纽约购车者将不必为已包含在车辆中的功能持续支付费用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/24/top_stories_11242026'><span class='en'>Nov 24</span><span class='zh'>11月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>773</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50622774' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>403</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50576012" data-id="50576012" data-tags="Web|Design|AI" data-score="770" data-comments="140" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50576012" data-time="1795188251">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://slopstrap.mikelacher.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Slopstrap – Make your site look hastily vibecoded</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap：让网站看起来像是匆忙用“氛围编程”写出来的</span></a><span class="dom">slopstrap.mikelacher.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slopstrap describes itself as a CSS framework intended to make websites look as though they were hastily created by an LLM.</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap 自称是一个 CSS 框架，旨在让网站看起来像是匆忙由 LLM 创建的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slopstrap describes itself as a CSS framework intended to make websites look as though they were hastily created by an LLM. It presents the tool as a way to give any project an intentionally AI-generated appearance.</span><span class='zh'>Slopstrap 自称是一个 CSS 框架，旨在让网站看起来像是匆忙由 LLM 创建的。它将自己定位为一种让项目呈现刻意 AI 生成风格的工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><span class='en'>Nov 20</span><span class='zh'>11月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>770</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50576012' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>140</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50542705" data-id="50542705" data-tags="AI" data-score="759" data-comments="684" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50542705" data-time="1794984446">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nicolasbouliane.com/blog/death-by-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nicolasbouliane.com/images/content2x/all-about-berlin-traffic-loss.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nicolasbouliane.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nicolasbouliane.com/blog/death-by-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is killing All About Berlin</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在扼杀 All About Berlin</span></a><span class="dom">nicolasbouliane.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The operator of All About Berlin says the site&#x27;s traffic fell by 70% to 75% after years of growth, attributing the decline to Google&#x27;s AI Overviews. Search pages now place an AI-generated answer trained on the site&#x27;s content before the link to the site itself. The change has had a devastating effect on the site&#x27;s traffic.</span><span class='zh'>All About Berlin 的运营者表示，该网站在多年增长后，流量因 Google AI Overviews 下跌了 70% 至 75%。如今搜索页面会先展示基于该网站内容训练的 AI 生成答案，最后才显示指向网站的链接。这一变化对网站流量造成了严重打击。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>759</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50542705' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>684</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50532613" data-id="50532613" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="756" data-comments="242" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50532613" data-time="1794928977">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.gov.br/receitafederal/pt-br/assuntos/noticias/2026/novembro/receita-federal-publica-primeiro-codigo-fonte-aberto-no-github-e-amplia-transparencia-da-reforma-tributaria" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gov.br&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gov.br</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">98</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gov.br/receitafederal/pt-br/assuntos/noticias/2026/novembro/receita-federal-publica-primeiro-codigo-fonte-aberto-no-github-e-amplia-transparencia-da-reforma-tributaria" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Brazil&#x27;s tax authority open-sources its tax calculator on GitHub under MIT</span><span class='zh'>巴西税务机关在 GitHub 以 MIT 许可证开源税务计算器</span></a><span class="dom">gov.br</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Brazil&#x27;s tax authority has open-sourced its tax calculator on GitHub under the MIT license.</span><span class='zh'>巴西税务机关已将其税务计算器以 MIT 许可证发布到 GitHub 上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Brazil&#x27;s tax authority has open-sourced its tax calculator on GitHub under the MIT license. The release makes the calculator&#x27;s source code available for public use and inspection.</span><span class='zh'>巴西税务机关已将其税务计算器以 MIT 许可证发布到 GitHub 上。此次发布让公众可以使用并检查该计算器的源代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TCP Use-After-Free: CVE-2026-98432 / CVSS 9.8</span><span class='zh'>TCP 释放后使用：CVE-2026-98432，CVSS 9.8</span></a><span class="dom">atlasum.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer. Stable kernels fixed it in October 2026, with patches for versions including 5.10.272 through 7.3, but the flaw dates back to Linux 4.4. At the reported time, Ubuntu still marked every release as vulnerable, while Red Hat listed RHEL 8, 9, and 10 as affected without a fix.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。稳定版内核已于 2026 年 10 月修复，补丁覆盖 5.10.272 至 7.3 等版本，但该缺陷可追溯至 Linux 4.4。报道所述时间点，Ubuntu 仍将所有版本标为易受攻击，Red Hat 也列出受影响的 RHEL 8、9 和 10，尚未提供修复。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gpupartner.com/blog/beginners-guide-local-ai-inkling-qwen-unsloth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Beginner&#x27;s Guide to Local AI with Inkling, Qwen, and Unsloth</span><span class='zh'>本地 AI 入门：Inkling、Qwen 与 Unsloth</span></a><span class="dom">gpupartner.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The guide explains how to set up a free, personal ChatGPT-like system on a home PC using Unsloth with Inkling or Qwen.</span><span class='zh'>这篇指南介绍了如何在家用电脑上使用 Unsloth 搭配 Inkling 或 Qwen，搭建免费的个人 ChatGPT 类系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The guide explains how to set up a free, personal ChatGPT-like system on a home PC using Unsloth with Inkling or Qwen. It emphasizes that the system can remain private and be shared across a household. The approach is presented as an alternative to cloud providers amid concerns about model control, quality changes, rising costs, and privacy.</span><span class='zh'>这篇指南介绍了如何在家用电脑上使用 Unsloth 搭配 Inkling 或 Qwen，搭建免费的个人 ChatGPT 类系统。该系统可以保持私密，并供全家使用。文章将这种方案视为云服务的替代选择，原因包括模型控制、质量变化、成本上涨和隐私问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase. It supports Rust, Python, JavaScript, React, C, and C++. The project describes its performance as twice that of GPUI.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。它支持 Rust、Python、JavaScript、React、C 和 C++。项目方称其性能是 GPUI 的两倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Gemini routes personal Drive files and emails to human annotators</span><span class='zh'>Google Gemini 将个人 Drive 文件和邮件交由人工标注员处理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini. This reverses a 2023 promise that personal Gmail, Docs, and Drive content would not be seen by human reviewers. Google now warns users not to connect apps containing confidential information they would not want a reviewer to see.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。这改变了 Google 在 2023 年作出的承诺——当时公司表示个人 Gmail、Docs 和 Drive 内容不会被人工审核员看到。Google 目前警告用户，不要连接包含不希望审核员看到的机密信息的应用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>716</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>147</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50438261" data-id="50438261" data-tags="Education|AI|Code" data-score="655" data-comments="155" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261" data-time="1794305902">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">135</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Socratic Tutor</span><span class='zh'>苏格拉底式导师</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture. It is intended for ongoing teaching sessions, not one-off factual questions. When the user’s intent is ambiguous, it first asks whether they want a quick answer or a full tutoring session.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。它适用于持续的教学过程，不适用于一次性的事实问答。当用户意图不明确时，它会先询问用户想要快速答案还是完整的辅导过程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>655</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>155</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50390124" data-id="50390124" data-tags="Privacy|Code" data-score="648" data-comments="424" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124" data-time="1793957232">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS. The report identifies app version 2.40.2 in the Netherlands. One user reported that the app appeared to work on a stock operating system.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。报告涉及荷兰地区的 2.40.2 版本。一名用户表示，该应用在原生操作系统上看起来可以正常运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3.1 Pro model weights are out</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3.1 Pro 模型权重已发布</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window. It was post-trained from Kimi K3 using large-scale joint reinforcement learning across hundreds of domains. The model replaces Gated MLA with Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention for more efficient long-context modeling.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。这是一款开放权重、原生多模态的智能体模型，拥有 2.8 万亿参数和 100 万 token 的上下文窗口。该模型基于 Kimi K3，并通过覆盖数百个领域的大规模联合强化学习进行后训练；同时以 Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention 替代 Gated MLA，以提高长上下文建模效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>627</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>186</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50607261" data-id="50607261" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware|Code" data-score="602" data-comments="244" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50607261" data-time="1795405764">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AFK-surf/xnulinux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>XNU Linux: Full OS X GUI Userspace on Linux</span><span class='zh'>XNU Linux：在 Linux 上运行完整的 OS X 图形用户空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>xnulinux builds Apple&#x27;s open-source XNU kernel as a loadable module inside an otherwise unmodified arm64 Linux kernel.</span><span class='zh'>xnulinux 将苹果开源的 XNU 内核构建为可加载模块，运行在未经修改的 arm64 Linux 内核中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>xnulinux builds Apple&#x27;s open-source XNU kernel as a loadable module inside an otherwise unmodified arm64 Linux kernel. The hosted XNU directly drives the CPU and MMU, allowing macOS processes to run natively alongside Linux processes without a hypervisor. Linux provides memory, timers, files, networking, and GPU services through a small interface module.</span><span class='zh'>xnulinux 将苹果开源的 XNU 内核构建为可加载模块，运行在未经修改的 arm64 Linux 内核中。运行于 Linux 内的 XNU 可直接驱动 CPU 和 MMU，使 macOS 进程无需虚拟机监控程序，就能与 Linux 进程在同一组 CPU 上原生运行。Linux 则通过一个小型接口模块为其提供内存、定时器、文件、网络和 GPU 服务。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/23/top_stories_11232026'><span class='en'>Nov 23</span><span class='zh'>11月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>602</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50607261' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>244</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50591853" data-id="50591853" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="582" data-comments="138" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50591853" data-time="1795284139">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ShinMegamiBoson/lookatmyquant" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ShinMegamiBoson/lookatmyquant" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inkling-2-Mini in 5.4 GB Memory</span><span class='zh'>在 5.4 GB 内存中运行 Inkling-2-Mini</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LookAtMyQuant is a quantization compiler that evaluates how much compressing each part of a model affects its output.</span><span class='zh'>LookAtMyQuant 是一个量化编译器，用于评估压缩模型各部分对输出造成的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LookAtMyQuant is a quantization compiler that evaluates how much compressing each part of a model affects its output. It selects codes for individual parts so the model fits a size budget with minimal damage. On Gemma 4 E2B, its GGUF reportedly achieves 12 times lower KL divergence than Q4_K_M at the same file size.</span><span class='zh'>LookAtMyQuant 是一个量化编译器，用于评估压缩模型各部分对输出造成的影响。它会为各个部分选择编码，使模型在满足大小限制的同时尽量减少损失。在 Gemma 4 E2B 上，其 GGUF 文件在相同文件大小下的 KL 散度据称比 Q4_K_M 低 12 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/21/top_stories_11212026'><span class='en'>Nov 21</span><span class='zh'>11月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>582</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50591853' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>138</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50284255" data-id="50284255" data-tags="Privacy|Data|AI|Code" data-score="536" data-comments="216" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255" data-time="1793244822">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code regularly sends each background agent’s transcript to its model</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会定期将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers. This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/glushchenko/fsnotes/discussions/2056" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple removed my open-source app from EU App Stores after 9 years</span><span class='zh'>苹果在欧盟 App Store 下架了我的开源应用，距发布已有 9 年</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The independent developer of FSNotes says Apple removed the open-source note-taking app from European App Stores after nine years.</span><span class='zh'>FSNotes 的独立开发者表示，苹果在该应用发布九年后将这款开源笔记应用从欧洲 App Store 下架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The independent developer of FSNotes says Apple removed the open-source note-taking app from European App Stores after nine years. FSNotes is available for macOS and iOS, and the developer says support requests have been ignored. The supplied excerpt does not state Apple&#x27;s reason for the removal.</span><span class='zh'>FSNotes 的独立开发者表示，苹果在该应用发布九年后将这款开源笔记应用从欧洲 App Store 下架。FSNotes 面向 macOS 和 iOS，开发者称自己的支持请求一直未获回应。现有摘录没有说明苹果下架该应用的原因。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers. It uses custom kernels, memory optimization, and weight streaming, with claims measured on the actual hardware. On a Xiaomi TV Box S 3rd Gen with about 1 GB of usable RAM, the project reports running models ranging from 260K to 16B parameters.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。项目使用定制内核、内存优化和权重流式传输，并在真实硬件上测量各项结果。在可用内存约 1 GB 的 Xiaomi TV Box S 第三代上，项目报告称可运行参数量从 26 万到 160 亿不等的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>466</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>180</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50320995" data-id="50320995" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="454" data-comments="229" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995" data-time="1793486772">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Memdoor: A team of 5 Qwen3.8-27B models beats Fable 5 on LiveCodeBench Hard</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor：五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 LiveCodeBench Hard 上击败 Fable 5</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker. Each model starts with a fresh context and communicates only through files, while an external script determines whether solutions pass. The authors reproduced the method using Qwen3.8-27B, eight YAML files, their prompts, and a 128K response limit.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。每个模型都从全新的上下文开始，仅通过磁盘文件通信，并由外部脚本判断解答是否通过。作者使用 Qwen3.8-27B、八个 YAML 文件、自定义提示词和 128K 回复上限复现了这一方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>454</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>229</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50539610" data-id="50539610" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="450" data-comments="98" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50539610" data-time="1794957529">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-research/google-research/tree/master/cogentic_research_papers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google releases Cogentic papers on integer factorization, discrete logs and more</span><span class='zh'>Google 发布关于整数分解、离散对数等问题的 Cogentic 论文</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Research has added a directory containing research papers produced by Cogentic.</span><span class='zh'>Google Research 新增了一个目录，收录由 Cogentic 产生的研究论文。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Research has added a directory containing research papers produced by Cogentic. The repository states that the papers are generally released under the Apache 2.0 license unless an individual subdirectory specifies otherwise.</span><span class='zh'>Google Research 新增了一个目录，收录由 Cogentic 产生的研究论文。仓库说明，除非具体子目录另有规定，这些论文通常以 Apache 2.0 许可证发布。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Get your coding agent off ~2019 JavaScript, onto modern web primitives</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让编码代理摆脱约 2019 年的 JavaScript，转向现代 Web 原语</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs. Examples include CSS highlights, dialog and showModal(), and field-sizing, which can replace common JavaScript-heavy patterns. The open-source release contains more than 200 expert-written guides, developed with Firefox, MDN, and Edge, and is designed for agent skills and retrieval-augmented tooling.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。例如，CSS Highlights、dialog 与 showModal()、field-sizing 都可以替代常见的重 JavaScript 实现。该开源项目目前包含 200 多份由专家编写的指南，并与 Firefox、MDN 和 Edge 合作开发，面向代理技能和检索增强工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>423</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50411961" data-id="50411961" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="404" data-comments="181" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961" data-time="1794133324">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/synchopate/llama-modchip" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip: 284B DeepSeek on 64GB RAM, no GPU</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip：仅用 64GB 内存、无需 GPU 运行 284B DeepSeek</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM. Its project page claims that a 284B DeepSeek-V4-Flash model can run on a 64GB CPU-only system at a 256K context length, with reported prompt-processing speeds of 44.4 tok/s and generation speeds of 9.1–14.7 tok/s. It supports x86_64 and ARM64 processors and also targets 30B MoE models.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。项目页面称，284B 参数的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 可在仅配备 64GB 内存、没有 GPU 的 CPU 系统上运行，支持 256K 上下文，提示处理速度为 44.4 token/s，生成速度为 9.1–14.7 token/s。该项目支持 x86_64 和 ARM64 处理器，也面向 30B MoE 模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>404</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>181</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50493062" data-id="50493062" data-tags="Programming|Hardware|Web|Code" data-score="399" data-comments="67" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50493062" data-time="1794623309">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ggml v1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>ggml v1.0.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support. It also delivers performance work across CUDA, Metal, Vulkan, SYCL, OpenCL, WebGPU, and Hexagon backends, including quantized matrix multiplication, MoE fusion, FlashAttention paths, and scheduler improvements. The release broadens hardware and backend support while reducing unnecessary input uploads.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。该版本还改进了 CUDA、Metal、Vulkan、SYCL、OpenCL、WebGPU 和 Hexagon 后端的性能，包括量化矩阵乘法、MoE 融合、FlashAttention 路径和调度器优化。此次发布扩大了硬件与后端支持，并减少了不必要的输入上传。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project called Leonida, described as a PC recompilation of the Xbox version of GTA 6, was banned from GitHub while its lead developer was still in school.</span><span class='zh'>名为 Leonida 的项目被描述为 GTA 6 Xbox 版本的 PC 重编译项目；该项目在首席开发者仍在上学期间被 GitHub 禁止。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GPUI is being moved into its own repository. It is an open-source, GPU-accelerated UI framework for Rust that combines immediate-mode and retained-mode approaches.</span><span class='zh'>GPUI 将迁移到独立的代码仓库中。它是一个面向 Rust 的开源 GPU 加速 UI 框架，结合了即时模式和保留模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>378</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>54</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50558906" data-id="50558906" data-tags="Security|Code" data-score="372" data-comments="95" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558906" data-time="1795088823">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/32781" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openjdk/jdk/pull/32781" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone reported java4shell bug in September 2026</span><span class='zh'>有人在 2026 年 9 月报告了 java4shell 漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An OpenJDK pull request reports a heap-corruption bug in the x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 decoder. The flaw appears when invalid input leaves a short padded tail, causing the decoder to calculate a negative output size and write beyond the logical output buffer before returning to Java. Valid inputs with lengths that are multiples of four do not trigger the issue.</span><span class='zh'>一份 OpenJDK 拉取请求报告了 x86-64 AVX-512 VBMI Base64 解码器中的堆损坏漏洞。当无效输入留下很短的填充尾部时，解码器可能计算出负的输出大小，并在返回 Java 之前写出逻辑输出缓冲区边界。长度为 4 的倍数的有效输入不会触发该问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>372</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50558906' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>95</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50537156" data-id="50537156" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="372" data-comments="199" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50537156" data-time="1794945040">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jquery/jquery/releases/tag/4.1.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0 was released, with the release notes listing an Ajax change that adds a responseURL field to jqXHR.</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0 已发布，更新说明列出了一项 Ajax 改动：为 jqXHR 添加 responseURL 字段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>jQuery 4.1.0 was released, with the release notes listing an Ajax change that adds a responseURL field to jqXHR. The same announcement also references a jQuery 3.8.0 release. The update continues development of the JavaScript library across its newer and older release lines.</span><span class='zh'>jQuery 4.1.0 已发布，更新说明列出了一项 Ajax 改动：为 jqXHR 添加 responseURL 字段。同一公告还提到了 jQuery 3.8.0 的发布。此次更新同时推进了该 JavaScript 库的新旧版本线。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openssl/openssl/pull/33652" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenSSL: Buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock() (CVE-2026-121481, CVSS 9.8)</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL：EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出（CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 9.8）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposed OpenSSL fix addresses a buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock(), tracked as CVE-2026-121481 with a CVSS score of 9.8.</span><span class='zh'>一项拟议的 OpenSSL 修复针对 EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出漏洞，该漏洞编号为 CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 评分为 9.8。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposed OpenSSL fix addresses a buffer overflow in EVP_EncodeBlock(), tracked as CVE-2026-121481 with a CVSS score of 9.8. The change makes the function return -1 for invalid inputs, including null buffers, negative lengths, and encoded sizes that do not fit in an int. The security advisory was still pending when the fix was proposed.</span><span class='zh'>一项拟议的 OpenSSL 修复针对 EVP_EncodeBlock() 中的缓冲区溢出漏洞，该漏洞编号为 CVE-2026-121481，CVSS 评分为 9.8。修复会让函数在输入无效时返回 -1，包括缓冲区为空、长度为负数或编码后大小无法放入 int 的情况。提出修复时，安全公告仍在等待发布。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><span class='en'>Nov 20</span><span class='zh'>11月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>365</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50569226' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>328</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50425017" data-id="50425017" data-tags="DevOps|Startups|Code" data-score="360" data-comments="145" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50425017" data-time="1794229364">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree. It provides a visual representation of systemd jobs during system startup.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。它在系统启动过程中以可视化方式呈现 systemd 作业。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gulivan/worker755" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a game where you are an LLM&#x27;s subagent</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个让你成为大语言模型子代理的游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Worker755 is a browser game in which an AI manager treats the player as its subagent.</span><span class='zh'>Worker755 是一款浏览器游戏，玩家在其中扮演一个被 AI 管理器视为子代理的角色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Worker755 is a browser game in which an AI manager treats the player as its subagent. The manager is a real LLM, such as Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek, GLM, or Kimi, with a task queue, read-only machine access, and a tool for spawning subagents. Players receive instructions in a terminal, have 90 seconds to act, and then receive feedback, corrections, incident reports, and a performance review.</span><span class='zh'>Worker755 是一款浏览器游戏，玩家在其中扮演一个被 AI 管理器视为子代理的角色。管理器使用真实的大语言模型，例如 Claude、GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、GLM 或 Kimi，并配有任务队列、只读的机器访问权限和生成子代理的工具。玩家会在终端收到指令，并有 90 秒执行；随后系统会反馈结果、提出修正、提交事故报告，最后给出绩效评估。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JiaFeiMiao-K-Cat/DiscImageStudio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DiscImageStudio: burn visible artwork onto CDs, DVDs and Blu-ray discs</span><span class='zh'>DiscImageStudio：将可见图案刻录到 CD、DVD 和蓝光光盘</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs. After burning, the disc surface displays a visible pattern. The Windows desktop version supports design and burning, while Linux and macOS have experimental offline command-line versions.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。Windows 桌面版支持设计与刻录，Linux 和 macOS 则提供实验性的离线命令行版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xoxorwr/pw-loudness-eq" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Windows Loudness Equalization, reverse-engineered and ported to PipeWire</span><span class='zh'>将 Windows 响度均衡反向工程并移植到 PipeWire</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project reverse-engineers Windows Loudness Equalization from WMALFXGFXDSP.dll and ports it to PipeWire.</span><span class='zh'>该项目从 WMALFXGFXDSP.dll 反向工程 Windows 的响度均衡功能，并将其移植到 PipeWire。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project reverse-engineers Windows Loudness Equalization from WMALFXGFXDSP.dll and ports it to PipeWire. It evens out each application&#x27;s volume so that quiet and loud parts play at more similar levels. It requires PipeWire 1.4 or newer and GTK 3 for its window.</span><span class='zh'>该项目从 WMALFXGFXDSP.dll 反向工程 Windows 的响度均衡功能，并将其移植到 PipeWire。它会平衡各应用的音量，让安静和响亮的部分以更接近的音量播放。项目需要 PipeWire 1.4 或更高版本，以及用于窗口界面的 GTK 3。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/saragordic/window-sweaters" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters is a macOS menu-bar app that adds knitted borders to application windows.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters is a macOS menu-bar app that adds knitted borders to application windows. It offers sweater-like borders and colors inspired by different apps. The project can also be handed to a coding agent to install.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。它提供类似毛衣的边框和受不同应用启发的配色。项目还可以交给编程代理来完成安装。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/tristanbuckmaster/navier-stokes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">402</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tristanbuckmaster/navier-stokes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations</span><span class='zh'>Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A repository contains the paper “Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations” by L.</span><span class='zh'>一个代码仓库收录了 L. Alpöge 和 T. Buckmaster 撰写的《Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解》，以及配套的 Lean 4 形式化证明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A repository contains the paper “Finite-time blow-up for the Navier–Stokes equations” by L. Alpöge and T. Buckmaster, together with an accompanying Lean 4 formalization. The formalization proves the stated theorems from a library while restricting the permitted axioms to propext, Quot.sound, and Classical.choice. Comparator files check that the theorem statements match the solutions.</span><span class='zh'>一个代码仓库收录了 L. Alpöge 和 T. Buckmaster 撰写的《Navier–Stokes 方程的有限时间爆破解》，以及配套的 Lean 4 形式化证明。该形式化代码通过一个库证明所述定理，并将允许使用的公理限制为 propext、Quot.sound 和 Classical.choice。比较文件用于检查定理陈述与解答是否一致。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><span class='en'>Nov 20</span><span class='zh'>11月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>315</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50576232' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>106</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50277379" data-id="50277379" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Web|Code" data-score="310" data-comments="108" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379" data-time="1793208563">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile solver library (no headless browser required)</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 求解库（无需无头浏览器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser. It returns a Turnstile token for widgets or a cf_clearance cookie for challenge pages. Widgets can be handled with a page URL and site key, while challenge pages require a direct fetch or the page’s HTML.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。它会为小部件返回 Turnstile 令牌，为挑战页面返回 cf_clearance Cookie。处理小部件需要页面 URL 和站点密钥，而挑战页面则需要直接抓取页面或提供页面 HTML。</span></p>
