<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ivankovic/omnidiff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniDiff – Opensource, super fast, content aware Git difftool, in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniDiff——用 Rust 编写的开源高速内容感知 Git 差异工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts. It can compare C++ files tens of thousands of lines long in under 100 milliseconds on a 2023 ThinkPad T14s. Its pipelines were informed by manually studying 2,000 diffs sampled from 7,400 Gentoo package repositories, and the project is currently at version 0.3.0.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。它在 2023 年款 ThinkPad T14s 上可在不到 100 毫秒内比较数万行的 C++ 文件。其处理流程源于对 7400 个 Gentoo 软件包仓库中抽样的 2000 个差异进行人工研究，目前版本为 0.3.0。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50325253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>94</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50454625" data-id="50454625" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="285" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625" data-time="1794411186">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings. It runs in the background without joining calls, and keeps transcripts, recordings, and summaries on the user’s computer. The app also offers calendar integration and a local MCP server for optionally sharing notes with external frontier models.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。它在后台运行，不会加入通话，会议转录、录音和摘要也不会离开用户的电脑。该应用还提供日历集成和本地 MCP 服务器，用户可以选择通过它将笔记分享给外部前沿模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>285</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50648050" data-id="50648050" data-tags="Programming|AI|Code" data-score="281" data-comments="127" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50648050" data-time="1795643290">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lsernau/grh-lean" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Much shorter version of GRH Lean verification from Anthropic</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 更短的 GRH Lean 形式化验证版本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact Lean 4 artifact formalizes proofs of the Riemann Hypothesis and the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for Dirichlet L-functions.</span><span class='zh'>一个精简的 Lean 4 项目形式化证明了黎曼猜想，以及关于狄利克雷 L 函数的广义黎曼猜想。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact Lean 4 artifact formalizes proofs of the Riemann Hypothesis and the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for Dirichlet L-functions. It is built on Mathlib and uses only the axioms propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound. The project is described as unmaintained and does not accept contributions.</span><span class='zh'>一个精简的 Lean 4 项目形式化证明了黎曼猜想，以及关于狄利克雷 L 函数的广义黎曼猜想。该项目基于 Mathlib，仅使用 propext、Classical.choice 和 Quot.sound 三个公理。项目说明称其不再维护，也不接受贡献。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hedgerow-dev/coldframe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Coldframe, a honeypot that poses as a supermarket fridge controller</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Coldframe，伪装成超市冰柜控制器的蜜罐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces. It uses only the Python standard library and matches details such as headers, page sizes, certificate fields, and TLS versions found on real devices. The honeypot recognizes several exploit attempts, including the CVE-2025-41450 login bypass, and records submitted codes.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。它仅使用 Python 标准库，并模拟真实设备的请求头、页面大小、证书字段和 TLS 版本等细节。该蜜罐能够识别多种攻击尝试，包括 CVE-2025-41450 登录绕过，并记录提交的代码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406292' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>64</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50610318" data-id="50610318" data-tags="Hardware|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="163" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50610318" data-time="1795433514">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/os6/nospeaker" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nospeaker ─ Make your MacBook&#x27;s trackpad talk using its haptic motor</span><span class='zh'>Nospeaker：用 MacBook 触控板的触觉马达发声</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nospeaker uses the MacBook trackpad&#x27;s haptic motor to generate audible sound, even when the computer&#x27;s speakers are muted.</span><span class='zh'>Nospeaker 利用 MacBook 触控板的触觉马达产生人耳可听见的声音，即使电脑扬声器处于静音状态也能工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nospeaker uses the MacBook trackpad&#x27;s haptic motor to generate audible sound, even when the computer&#x27;s speakers are muted. It drives the motor at up to 4,000 taps per second, fast enough to produce sound that can be heard. The project turns the trackpad into a voice, singing, rapping, or musical instrument.</span><span class='zh'>Nospeaker 利用 MacBook 触控板的触觉马达产生人耳可听见的声音，即使电脑扬声器处于静音状态也能工作。它以每秒最高 4000 次的频率驱动马达，速度足以发声。该项目可以让触控板说话、唱歌、说唱，甚至变成乐器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/23/top_stories_11232026'><span class='en'>Nov 23</span><span class='zh'>11月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50610318' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>163</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50306442" data-id="50306442" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Hardware|Gaming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442" data-time="1793378422">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A working Nintendo in ~1GB of Qwen weights</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在约 1GB 的 Qwen 权重中运行一台可工作的任天堂主机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model. The NES 6502 CPU, 2 KB of work RAM, and cartridge ROM run inside the model checkpoint, with every weight computed by a compiler rather than learned through training. During play, the model predicts the CPU execution log token by token while a host program handles graphics, controls, and the vertical-blank interrupt.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。NES 的 6502 CPU、2 KB 工作内存和卡带程序 ROM 都运行在这个模型检查点内，所有权重均由编译器计算得出，而不是通过训练学习。游戏运行时，模型逐 token 预测 CPU 执行日志，主机程序则负责绘图、读取手柄输入和触发垂直消隐中断。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>280</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50348962" data-id="50348962" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Programming|Data|Code" data-score="274" data-comments="132" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50348962" data-time="1793689490">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/auto-ontology" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">462</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/auto-ontology" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia – open-source auto-ontology</span><span class='zh'>Nvidia：开源自动本体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data. It reconciles enterprise metadata and definitions across systems, helping agents locate, interpret, and retrieve data more reliably. The layer is exposed to agent systems through MCP, CLI, and API, and supports text-to-SQL evaluation workflows.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。它整合企业元数据并协调不同系统中的定义，帮助智能体更可靠地定位、理解和检索数据。该推理层可通过 MCP、CLI 和 API 接入智能体，也支持 text-to-SQL 评测流程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>274</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50348962' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>132</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497589" data-id="50497589" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="272" data-comments="27" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497589" data-time="1794668655">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vaibhaav-Tiwari/deepseek-v3-jax" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DeepSeek V3 in ~200 lines of pure Jax</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用约 200 行纯 JAX 实现 DeepSeek V3</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project implements the DeepSeek-V3 architecture in 190 lines of pure JAX, including MLA, DeepSeekMoE, auxiliary-loss-free balancing, and multi-token prediction. The model was pretrained from scratch on a MacBook Air using public-domain detective fiction, without PyTorch, Flax, or Equinox. Its parameters are plain dictionaries of arrays, and the implementation is organized as readable functions.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目用 190 行纯 JAX 实现了 DeepSeek-V3 架构，包括 MLA、DeepSeekMoE、无辅助损失均衡和多词元预测。该模型在 MacBook Air 上使用公版侦探小说从头预训练，未使用 PyTorch、Flax 或 Equinox。模型参数采用由数组组成的普通字典，代码则组织为可从头读到尾的函数。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pylance-release/blob/main/docs/tsp/type-server-protocol.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type Server Protocol</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，扩展语言服务器协议</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol. The protocol enables editors and language servers to exchange richer typing information and state, improving performance and scalability for multi-language toolchains. This move could help unify editor experiences across languages and tooling ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。此举有望提升多语言工具链的性能与可扩展性，并统一编辑器体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr released a native app, expanding beyond its earlier web or cross-platform offering and delivering faster performance, offline support, and deeper OS integration.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。</span></p>

The move signals a maturation in the product and potential for broader platform reach.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。此举标志着产品成熟度提升，并可能带来更广泛的应用平台覆盖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikuta0407/win4wine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Win4wine – Run Windows Shell as the Linux Desktop on Wine</span><span class='zh'>Win4wine：在 Wine 上把 Windows Shell 作为 Linux 桌面运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Win4wine runs Windows XP&#x27;s explorer.exe as a Linux desktop shell on a patched version of Wine.</span><span class='zh'>Win4wine 让 Windows XP 的 explorer.exe 在打过补丁的 Wine 上充当 Linux 桌面 Shell。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Win4wine runs Windows XP&#x27;s explorer.exe as a Linux desktop shell on a patched version of Wine. It provides the original XP taskbar, Start menu, desktop, and folder windows, while Linux applications can appear inside XP-style frames and use its taskbar. The project is experimental, has only been tested on a fresh Debian 13 installation, and is not yet complete enough for everyday use.</span><span class='zh'>Win4wine 让 Windows XP 的 explorer.exe 在打过补丁的 Wine 上充当 Linux 桌面 Shell。它提供 XP 原版的任务栏、开始菜单、桌面和文件夹窗口，Linux 应用也能显示在 XP 风格的窗口和任务栏中。该项目仍属实验性质，目前只在全新安装的 Debian 13 上测试过，尚未达到日常使用的成熟度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/da-luce/astroterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astroterm: A Planetarium for Your Terminal</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm：让你的终端成为行星仪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell. It brings rich visualization to environments without a full GUI, making it a handy tool for quick stargazing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。即使在无完整图形界面的环境下，也能进行直观的天文可视化，适合快速观星与学习。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SheepSeb/tinyworldformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tiny world model running games on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上运行的微型世界模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers. The demo demonstrates memory-efficient planning and small neural models that fit in ultra‑compact hardware, hinting at future autonomous nodes in IoT. It signals potential for smarter, low-power devices at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。该演示表明在资源受限设备上也能运行简短的神经模型和规划任务。预示着物联网边缘设备将拥有更智能的能力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/sous-labs/songe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1465322684/f6c97c41-6e40-4e48-83c6-1b20210672d6' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">567</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sous-labs/songe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A training engine for your local model, using your idle compute</span><span class='zh'>利用本地模型空闲算力的训练引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Songe is a training engine that uses a local model’s idle time to learn from what happens after each action, including tool results, errors, and the user’s next message.</span><span class='zh'>Songe 是一个利用本地模型空闲时间进行训练的引擎，会从每次操作之后发生的事情中学习，包括工具结果、错误和用户的下一条消息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Songe is a training engine that uses a local model’s idle time to learn from what happens after each action, including tool results, errors, and the user’s next message. It works with local models through opencode, pi, or other OpenAI-compatible tools. New model versions are used only after passing checks, and the project says data never leaves the machine.</span><span class='zh'>Songe 是一个利用本地模型空闲时间进行训练的引擎，会从每次操作之后发生的事情中学习，包括工具结果、错误和用户的下一条消息。它可通过 opencode、pi 或其他兼容 OpenAI 的工具使用本地模型。新版本只有通过检查后才会启用，项目称数据不会离开本机。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Process Explorer for macOS</span><span class='zh'>适用于 macOS 的 Process Explorer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience. It provides a live process tree, module, handle, and thread inspection, per-process properties, system-wide performance graphs, and code-signing checks. The Swift, SwiftUI, and AppKit application uses public macOS APIs, with optional support for a privileged helper.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。它提供实时进程树、模块、句柄和线程检查、逐进程属性、系统级性能图表以及代码签名检查。该应用使用 Swift、SwiftUI 和 AppKit 构建，并通过 macOS 公共 API 工作，还可选择使用特权辅助程序。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。</span></p>

It’s a thought-provoking take on making debugging less mechanical and more narrative.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。它挑战传统的机械式排错，试图让调试过程更具叙事性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pithings/unkernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A real Linux kernel, compiled to WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>真正的 Linux 内核，编译为 WebAssembly</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unkernel compiles a real Linux kernel to WebAssembly.</span><span class='zh'>Unkernel 将真正的 Linux 内核编译成 WebAssembly。</span></p>

The project also provides performance information, feature documentation, benchmarks, and a JavaScript SDK.</span><span class='zh'>Unkernel 将真正的 Linux 内核编译成 WebAssembly。该内核启动到 shell 所需时间不到一秒。项目还提供性能信息、功能文档、基准测试和 JavaScript SDK。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">676</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NariQ: BF16-level Qwen3.8-27B in a 10 GB file</span><span class='zh'>NariQ：10 GB 文件中的 BF16 级 Qwen3.8-27B</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NariQ provides 3-bit-or-lower quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B that its creators say retain near-BF16 quality across agentic tasks, mathematics, multilingual reasoning, and image understanding.</span><span class='zh'>NariQ 提供 Qwen3.8-27B 的 3 bit 及更低位宽量化版本，其开发者称这些版本在智能体任务、数学、多语言推理和图像理解方面保持接近 BF16 的质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NariQ provides 3-bit-or-lower quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B that its creators say retain near-BF16 quality across agentic tasks, mathematics, multilingual reasoning, and image understanding. Their benchmarks report similar or sometimes higher scores than BF16 at 3-bit on DeepSWE, Terminal-Bench, AIME, MSGM, and RealWorldQA, while 2-bit performance drops more noticeably on some tasks. The quantized models require a fork of llama.cpp, and decoding speed is reported as comparable to other quantizations on an RTX PRO 6000. Context-length tests cover 4K, 16K, and 64K tokens, with lower-bit models generally decoding faster.</span><span class='zh'>NariQ 提供 Qwen3.8-27B 的 3 bit 及更低位宽量化版本，其开发者称这些版本在智能体任务、数学、多语言推理和图像理解方面保持接近 BF16 的质量。基准测试显示，3 bit 版本在 DeepSWE、Terminal-Bench、AIME、MSGM 和 RealWorldQA 上的成绩与 BF16 相近，部分项目甚至更高，而 2 bit 版本在部分任务上的下降更明显。量化模型需要使用 llama.cpp 的一个分支，开发者称其在 RTX PRO 6000 上的解码速度与其他量化版本相当。上下文长度测试覆盖 4K、16K 和 64K token，较低位宽模型通常解码更快。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/23/top_stories_11232026'><span class='en'>Nov 23</span><span class='zh'>11月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>192</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50611402' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>36</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50299811" data-id="50299811" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="186" data-comments="32" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299811" data-time="1793333826">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nibnib: Image compression with extreme decompression speed</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib：解压速度极快的图像压缩格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression. It began as an experiment targeting DVI output on an RP2350 microcontroller, where roughly six cycles per pixel must cover pixel fetching, palette lookup, and storage in a line buffer.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。它最初是为 RP2350 微控制器上的 DVI 输出进行的实验，该设备每个像素约有 6 个时钟周期，需要完成像素读取、调色板查询和写入行缓冲区。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>186</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299811' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>32</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50596155" data-id="50596155" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="179" data-comments="149" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50596155" data-time="1795320609">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">719</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EL4CTEO/fast-anything" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything: Don&#x27;t rewrite it in Rust. Rewrite the 5% that&#x27;s slow</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything：别用 Rust 重写全部，只重写最慢的 5%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything profiles Python, Node, or Ruby applications, ports their hottest functions with a coding agent, and verifies that behavior remains unchanged.</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything 会分析 Python、Node 或 Ruby 应用的性能热点，用编程代理移植最慢的函数，并验证行为是否保持一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fast-Anything profiles Python, Node, or Ruby applications, ports their hottest functions with a coding agent, and verifies that behavior remains unchanged. Its premise is that rewriting an entire application in Rust can take months, while most runtime is concentrated in a small number of functions. The tool therefore targets only the slowest 5% rather than replacing the whole application.</span><span class='zh'>Fast-Anything 会分析 Python、Node 或 Ruby 应用的性能热点，用编程代理移植最慢的函数，并验证行为是否保持一致。它的出发点是，整个应用用 Rust 重写可能耗时数月，而大部分运行时间通常集中在少数函数上。因此，该工具只针对最慢的 5%，而不是替换整个应用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>179</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50596155' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>149</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50233059" data-id="50233059" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="166" data-comments="156" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233059" data-time="1792944356">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/e-n-f/if-then-else/blob/master/if-then-else.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If-then-else had to be invented (2020)</span><span class='zh'>if-then-else 需要被发明吗（2020）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>一篇关于在编程语言设计中 if-then-else 条件表达式为何重要的讨论性文章，回顾历史背景并探讨潜在替代方案，质疑我们如何在代码中组织逻辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A speculative piece arguing about why conditional constructs like if-then-else are essential in programming language design, exploring historical aspects and potential alternatives. It challenges assumptions about how we structure logic in code.</span><span class='zh'>一篇关于在编程语言设计中 if-then-else 条件表达式为何重要的讨论性文章，回顾历史背景并探讨潜在替代方案，质疑我们如何在代码中组织逻辑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Unleash/unleash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;ve built an open-source feature flag server for 11 years</span><span class='zh'>我们打造开源功能开关服务器已有 11 年</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released. It supports smaller, more manageable production deployments and can streamline software delivery. The project has been developed for 11 years.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。它支持更小、更易管理的生产部署，并可简化软件交付流程。该项目已开发 11 年。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/datareceipts/data-receipts/tree/main/109-gpu-pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The same H100 costs $2.69 or $12.29 an hour depending where you rent it</span><span class='zh'>同一块 H100 的租用价格可能是每小时 2.69 或 12.29 美元</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour. For identical GPUs, it reports a 4.57× gap between the cheapest and most expensive options. Running one GPU continuously for a month costs $1,964 or $8,972 depending only on the provider.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。对于相同 GPU，最便宜和最昂贵选项之间的价格差达到 4.57 倍。连续运行一块 GPU 一个月，费用可能因服务商不同而为 1964 美元或 8972 美元。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anomalyco/rune" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rune: A native GPU-rendered Rust UI framework for macOS, Windows, Linux and web</span><span class='zh'>Rune：面向 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web 的原生 GPU 渲染 Rust UI 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rune is a native UI framework for Rust that uses GPU rendering across macOS, Windows, Linux, and the web.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个使用 GPU 渲染的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，支持 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rune is a native UI framework for Rust that uses GPU rendering across macOS, Windows, Linux, and the web. It provides its own renderer, text system, layout engine, and spring-based animations. Applications can also run headlessly, allowing agents to render PNG images, dump the element tree, and drive scripted input.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个使用 GPU 渲染的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，支持 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web。它自带渲染器、文本系统、布局引擎和基于弹簧模型的动画。应用还可以无头运行，使智能体能够渲染 PNG、导出元素树并通过脚本驱动输入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sebaandrada-cell/PcapAnalyzer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer – self-hosted pcap analyzer with 27 problem detectors</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer：带 27 种问题检测器的自托管 pcap 分析器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer analyzes .pcap, .pcapng, and gzip-compressed captures and identifies what is failing, why, and what to check next.</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer 可分析 .pcap、.pcapng 以及 gzip 压缩的抓包文件，并指出故障所在、原因和后续检查方向。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PcapAnalyzer analyzes .pcap, .pcapng, and gzip-compressed captures and identifies what is failing, why, and what to check next. It includes 27 problem detectors and AI-assisted investigation. The open-source Apache 2.0 tool runs locally, so captures do not leave the network, and it is currently in beta.</span><span class='zh'>PcapAnalyzer 可分析 .pcap、.pcapng 以及 gzip 压缩的抓包文件，并指出故障所在、原因和后续检查方向。它包含 27 种问题检测器，并支持 AI 辅助调查。这个采用 Apache 2.0 许可的开源工具在本地运行，因此抓包数据不会离开网络，目前仍处于测试阶段。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonewolf-im/lonewolf/releases/tag/v0.3.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3 – A standalone XMPP server written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3：用 Rust 编写的独立 XMPP 服务器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 adds certificate-authenticated federation, direct TLS, and additional desktop-client features. It remains a pre-1.0, single-node server, while full XMPP compliance and clustering are still on the roadmap. The release expands secure federation without yet providing those larger deployment features.</span><span class='zh'>Lonewolf 0.3.0 新增基于证书认证的联邦通信、直接 TLS，以及更多桌面客户端功能。它仍是一个处于 1.0 之前阶段的单节点服务器，完整 XMPP 兼容性和集群支持仍在路线图中。此次更新增强了安全联邦能力，但尚未提供这些更大规模部署功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bigcrimping/floppy3.5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Floppy3.5: A portable 3.5-inch floppy WiFi NAS</span><span class='zh'>Floppy3.5：便携式 3.5 英寸软盘 Wi‑Fi NAS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Floppy3.5 is a portable Wi-Fi NAS that reads 3.5-inch floppy disks, including disks used by older digital cameras such as the Sony Mavica.</span><span class='zh'>Floppy3.5 是一款便携式 Wi‑Fi NAS，可读取包括 Sony Mavica 等老式数码相机使用的 3.5 英寸软盘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Floppy3.5 is a portable Wi-Fi NAS that reads 3.5-inch floppy disks, including disks used by older digital cameras such as the Sony Mavica. It displays files and photos through a web page and can copy them to a microSD card. The device supports FAT12 1.44 MB HD disks.</span><span class='zh'>Floppy3.5 是一款便携式 Wi‑Fi NAS，可读取包括 Sony Mavica 等老式数码相机使用的 3.5 英寸软盘。它能通过网页显示文件和照片，并将其复制到 microSD 卡。该设备支持 FAT12 1.44 MB 高密度软盘。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/IRS-Public/direct-file" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US IRS free online tax filing open source repo (2025)</span><span class='zh'>美国国税局免费在线报税开源仓库（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Direct File is a US government service that lets taxpayers file federal returns electronically for free and directly with the IRS. It uses interview-style questions, works across phones and computers, supports English and Spanish, and translates users&#x27; answers into tax forms submitted through the IRS Modernized e-File API. The repository contains the service&#x27;s code and related tests and scenarios.</span><span class='zh'>Direct File 是美国政府提供的服务，允许纳税人免费通过电子方式直接向美国国税局提交联邦税表。该服务采用问答式界面，可在手机和电脑等设备上使用，支持英语和西班牙语，并将用户答案转换为税表后通过国税局 Modernized e-File API 提交。该开源仓库包含服务代码以及相关测试和场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>148</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50499083' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>91</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50246238" data-id="50246238" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="148" data-comments="34" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50246238" data-time="1793031149">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/hob" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hob - Open-source, on device alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT</span><span class='zh'>Hob：开源的本地替代，面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally. It demonstrates the feasibility of private, offline inference on consumer hardware, though model size and compute demands remain practical constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。它展示了在消费级硬件上实现私有、离线推理的可行性，但模型规模与计算需求仍是实际制约。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/elixir-hls/elixir-hls" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Elixir HLS makes FPGA circuits from functions written in Elixir</span><span class='zh'>Elixir HLS：用 Elixir 函数生成 FPGA 电路</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Elixir HLS is a high-level synthesis tool that generates FPGA circuits from functions written in Elixir.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir HLS 是一个高层次综合工具，可从 Elixir 编写的函数生成 FPGA 电路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Elixir HLS is a high-level synthesis tool that generates FPGA circuits from functions written in Elixir. Unlike RTL development in Verilog or VHDL, users describe what the circuit should compute while the tool chooses clocks and registers. The generated result is RTL rather than manually written register-level code.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir HLS 是一个高层次综合工具，可从 Elixir 编写的函数生成 FPGA 电路。与使用 Verilog 或 VHDL 编写 RTL 不同，用户只需描述电路要计算的内容，由工具决定时钟和寄存器。最终生成的结果仍是 RTL，只是不再需要手工编写寄存器级代码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/23/top_stories_11232026'><span class='en'>Nov 23</span><span class='zh'>11月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>147</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50584526' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50294506" data-id="50294506" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Security|Code" data-score="145" data-comments="108" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294506" data-time="1793302064">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johncassil/dontallow" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Auto Dismiss Mac OS &quot;Allow &lt;app&gt; to find devices on local networks?&quot;</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：自动拒绝 MacOS 提示“允许应用发现本地网络设备？”</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt. It highlights the tension between a smooth UX and strict local-network permissions, inviting a discussion on safety vs convenience. The project serves as a reminder of how permission prompts influence everyday security decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？”提示，提升了使用体验。产品背后的讨论聚焦于 UX 与本地网络权限之间的权衡，以及安全治理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tylertms/Stela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A general purpose Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs</span><span class='zh'>面向 x86-64 PC 的通用 Apple Silicon 层</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs. It includes an Arm64-to-x86-64 translator, an NVIDIA Metal driver, and a model of Apple silicon hardware, and can currently boot macOS and most apps. The v0.1.0 release is slow, requires more testing, and has a low chance of booting on current hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。它包含 Arm64 到 x86-64 的转换器、NVIDIA Metal 驱动以及 Apple 芯片硬件模型，目前可以启动 macOS 和大多数应用。v0.1.0 运行速度较慢，仍需大量测试，在当前硬件上成功启动的概率也很低。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>140</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50449891" data-id="50449891" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI|Code" data-score="138" data-comments="60" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891" data-time="1794373007">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adrbn/bo2-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Black Ops II statically recompiled to native ARM64 macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator. The game reportedly runs at nearly 60 frames per second across the campaign, local multiplayer with bots, and local Zombies mode, including owned DLC maps. Split-screen is being worked on, while LAN mode is planned for later.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。据称，战役、本地机器人多人模式和本地僵尸模式都能以接近 60 帧运行，已拥有的 DLC 地图也可使用。开发者正在处理分屏功能，局域网模式则计划稍后加入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/snipedbywifi/terminactory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A terminal-native idle factory game, written in Rust with ratatui</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 和 ratatui 编写的终端原生放置工厂游戏</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks. It includes basic factory mechanics such as building, deleting, selecting, measuring earnings, and researching unlocks. Additional features include a fictional browser with a wiki and forums, extensive lore, and hooks that display coding-agent responses as in-game “telegrams.”</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。游戏包含建造、删除、选择、收益测算和研究解锁等基本工厂机制。它还加入了带有 Wiki 和论坛的虚构浏览器、大量背景故事，以及将编码代理回复显示为游戏内“电报”的钩子功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/MACaQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Huawei releases MACaQ, its internal quantizer for local inference</span><span class='zh'>华为发布用于本地推理的内部量化器 MACaQ</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models. It supports llama.cpp, vLLM, Diffusers, and PyTorch. Huawei says it can quantize a 122-billion-parameter model in 64 minutes using 32.5 GB of device memory, while achieving lower perplexity than MR-GPTQ and DuQuant++ in stated comparisons.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。它兼容 llama.cpp、vLLM、Diffusers 和 PyTorch。华为称，MACaQ 可使用 32.5 GB 设备内存，在 64 分钟内完成一个 1220 亿参数模型的量化；在其给出的比较中，困惑度低于 MR-GPTQ 和 DuQuant++。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>132</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50410249" data-id="50410249" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="131" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410249" data-time="1794109639">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dmunozv04/isponsorblocktv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ISponsorBlockTV: SponsorBlock client for all YouTube TV clients</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV：适用于所有 YouTube TV 客户端的 SponsorBlock 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API. It can also mute videos automatically and press YouTube&#x27;s &quot;Skip Ad&quot; button when it appears. YouTube appears to have changed its screen-ID format and is revoking existing codes, so devices must be paired again.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。它还可以自动静音，并在 YouTube 广告出现“跳过广告”按钮后自动点击。YouTube 似乎更改了屏幕 ID 格式并正在撤销现有代码，因此设备需要重新配对。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/otya128/winevdm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>16-bit Windows (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0, 3.1, etc.) on 64-bit Windows</span><span class='zh'>在64位 Windows 上运行16位 Windows 应用（1.x/2.x/3.0/3.1 等）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime. It illustrates how modern Windows still lacks native 16-bit support, leaving preservation-focused projects to recreate the old environment. This matters for running legacy software, archaeology of software, and niche users who still rely on early Windows programs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。与现代 Windows 不再原生支持16位应用不同，兼容层/运行时的实现让老旧软件得以保存与使用，但仍存在兼容性与性能限制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/27/top_stories_10272026'><span class='en'>Oct 27</span><span class='zh'>10月27日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50275128" data-id="50275128" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="126" data-comments="23" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50275128" data-time="1793200673">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/nvidia/nemotron-transcribe-granary" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe: open ASR for 40 languages</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe：40 种语言的开源 ASR</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications. The release lets researchers compare performance against other open models and accelerates progress in open speech AI.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。该发布使研究者能够与其它开源模型进行对比，加速开放语音 AI 的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/onlook-dev/onlook/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Onlook (YC W25) – An open-source developer tool for designers</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Onlook（YC W25）——面向设计师的开源开发工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Onlook is an open-source desktop app that places a design canvas over a real codebase, allowing designers to work directly in code with an existing agent. Users can work in multiple simultaneous worktrees running the actual app locally, with changes submitted as reviewable or discardable pull requests. The project says more than 25,000 designers and developers have supported it and over 100 people have contributed.</span><span class='zh'>Onlook 是一款开源桌面应用，在真实代码库之上提供设计画布，让设计师可以借助现有代理直接进行代码设计。用户可以在多个同时运行真实应用的工作树中操作，并将每次修改提交为可审查或可丢弃的拉取请求。项目方称已有超过 25,000 名设计师和开发者支持该项目，贡献者超过 100 人。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/19/top_stories_11192026'><span class='en'>Nov 19</span><span class='zh'>11月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>125</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50562320' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>85</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50387738" data-id="50387738" data-tags="DevOps|Hardware|Code" data-score="123" data-comments="27" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50387738" data-time="1793934986">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dsx-ai-factory/nvke" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Kubernetes Engine (NVKE)</span><span class='zh'>Nvidia Kubernetes Engine（NVKE）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine is a management plane for running Kubernetes on bare-metal NVIDIA GPU infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine 是一个用于在裸机 NVIDIA GPU 基础设施上运行 Kubernetes 的管理平面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine is a management plane for running Kubernetes on bare-metal NVIDIA GPU infrastructure. Platform teams can install it to create and operate workload clusters, each with its own control plane, etcd, and node pools. NVKE is currently a preview release, and its APIs, configuration, and behavior may change, so it is not recommended for production workloads.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine 是一个用于在裸机 NVIDIA GPU 基础设施上运行 Kubernetes 的管理平面。平台团队可以安装它来创建和运营工作负载集群，每个集群都拥有独立的控制平面、etcd 和节点池。NVKE 目前仍处于预览阶段，API、配置和行为可能发生变化，因此不建议用于生产工作负载。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A paper claims that the polynomial Hirsch conjecture is false.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文宣称，多项式 Hirsch 猜想是错误的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A paper claims that the polynomial Hirsch conjecture is false.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文宣称，多项式 Hirsch 猜想是错误的。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aryamandarda/tamserve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Open-source, real-time object tracking for live video feeds</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：面向实时视频流的开源实时目标跟踪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tamserve runs Meta’s SAM 3 on live camera feeds, detecting named objects and tracking them across multiple cameras. On an RTX 4090, it is reported to be four times faster than SAM 3’s tracker for arbitrary objects while retaining 92% of its accuracy; for people, it can handle 16 cameras concurrently. Its memory use stays around 3.5 GB on a 3,000-frame video, while SAM 3 runs out of memory at frame 423.</span><span class='zh'>Tamserve 在实时摄像头画面上运行 Meta 的 SAM 3，可根据名称发现目标，并在多个摄像头之间持续跟踪。在 RTX 4090 上，对于任意目标，其速度据称是 SAM 3 跟踪器的 4 倍，同时保持 92% 的准确率；针对人员，它可以同时处理 16 个摄像头。处理 3,000 帧视频时，其内存占用保持在约 3.5 GB，而 SAM 3 在第 423 帧就会耗尽内存。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>119</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50523377' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50381687" data-id="50381687" data-tags="Startups|Open Source|DevOps|Code" data-score="118" data-comments="58" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50381687" data-time="1793899062">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/rivet-dev/rivet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/654560183/d4b2fdae-1a15-4420-a2a3-17256a51ae24' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#fb7185' data-tag='Startups'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rivet-dev/rivet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Rivet (YC W23) – Scale and Orchestrate Agents on S3</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Rivet（YC W23）——在 S3 上扩展和编排代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rivet is an open-source, self-hostable orchestrator for agent workloads built around lightweight actors backed by S3.</span><span class='zh'>Rivet 是一个开源、可自行托管的代理工作负载编排器，围绕由 S3 支持的轻量级 actor 构建。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rivet is an open-source, self-hostable orchestrator for agent workloads built around lightweight actors backed by S3. Each actor provides a long-lived process, in-memory context, a WebSocket server, durable SQLite storage backed by S3, and idle hibernation. The team says the design grew out of infrastructure work for multiplayer games and aims to avoid the overhead of running a heavyweight sandbox per agent.</span><span class='zh'>Rivet 是一个开源、可自行托管的代理工作负载编排器，围绕由 S3 支持的轻量级 actor 构建。每个 actor 都提供长期运行的进程、内存上下文、WebSocket 服务器、由 S3 支持的持久化 SQLite 存储，以及空闲休眠功能。团队表示，该设计源自多人游戏基础设施工作，旨在避免为每个代理运行重量级沙箱的开销。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/diggerhq/opencomputer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OpenComputer – an open source alternative to Devin</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OpenComputer——Devin 的开源替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenComputer is an open-source cloud coding agent for teams that can run in a company’s AWS, GCP, or Azure account.</span><span class='zh'>OpenComputer 是一个面向团队的开源云端编码代理，可以运行在企业自己的 AWS、GCP 或 Azure 账户中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenComputer is an open-source cloud coding agent for teams that can run in a company’s AWS, GCP, or Azure account. Its agent harness and sandbox share a durable VM, while sessions are isolated in Git worktrees and backed up after every turn. The authors report a first commit time of 24 seconds, compared with 53–72 seconds for alternatives they benchmarked, and say a continuously running VM can become cheaper at higher usage.</span><span class='zh'>OpenComputer 是一个面向团队的开源云端编码代理，可以运行在企业自己的 AWS、GCP 或 Azure 账户中。它将代理工具链和沙箱运行在同一台持久化虚拟机上，使用 Git worktree 隔离会话，并在每轮操作后备份会话文件。作者报告称其首次提交耗时 24 秒，而对比方案为 53 至 72 秒；在较高使用量下，持续运行的虚拟机也可能更便宜。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/20/top_stories_11202026'><span class='en'>Nov 20</span><span class='zh'>11月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>118</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50581403' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>36</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50340992" data-id="50340992" data-tags="Programming|AI|Web|Code" data-score="117" data-comments="37" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340992" data-time="1793643039">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aaitor/skill-video-studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Skill to Create Product Videos</span><span class='zh'>创建产品视频的技能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project is a Claude Code skill for creating product videos.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用于制作产品视频的 Claude Code 技能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project is a Claude Code skill for creating product videos. It supports real-browser capture, branded composition, and local mastering, and includes a quick start, worked videos, and usage instructions.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用于制作产品视频的 Claude Code 技能。它支持真实浏览器录制、品牌化合成和本地母版制作，并提供快速入门、示例视频和使用说明。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kurtzer&#x27;s Ginux is now a userspace kernel</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kurtzer 的 Ginux 现已成为用户态内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances. It runs as an ordinary Linux process and defines an explicit, versioned contract between managed workloads and the host kernel. Its earlier phase as an independent Rust kernel booting through UEFI is complete, with that source preserved separately while active development continues on the userspace version.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。它作为普通 Linux 进程运行，并在受管理工作负载与主机内核之间建立明确且有版本控制的契约。此前通过 UEFI 启动、以 Rust 编写的独立内核阶段已经结束，相关源代码被单独保留，当前开发转向用户态版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>114</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>76</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50496787" data-id="50496787" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Web|Data|Education|Code" data-score="112" data-comments="68" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50496787" data-time="1794662230">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ZigaoWang/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Zigzag, a native Mac browser for people leaving Arc</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Zigzag——为离开 Arc 的用户打造的原生 Mac 浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zigzag is a free, open-source Mac browser written in Swift and based on WebKit, created by a high-school student from Shanghai. It aims to provide an Arc-like interface and can import spaces, pins, history, passwords, and cookies from Arc or other browsers. Despite using WebKit, it supports Chrome extensions, including 1Password, Bitwarden, and Tampermonkey. The browser requires no account and claims to have no telemetry or analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Zigzag 是一款由上海高中生开发的免费开源 Mac 浏览器，使用 Swift 编写并基于 WebKit。它试图提供类似 Arc 的界面，并可从 Arc 或其他浏览器导入空间、固定项目、历史记录、密码和 Cookie。虽然基于 WebKit，它仍支持 Chrome 扩展，包括 1Password、Bitwarden 和 Tampermonkey。该浏览器无需账户，并声称不包含遥测或分析功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/eclipse-mosquitto/mosquitto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto – An open source MQTT broker</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto：开源 MQTT 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Eclipse Mosquitto is an open-source implementation of an MQTT broker supporting MQTT versions 5.0, 3.1.1, and 3.1. It also provides C and C++ client libraries and command-line tools for publishing, subscribing, and administering the broker.</span><span class='zh'>Eclipse Mosquitto 是一个开源 MQTT 代理实现，支持 MQTT 5.0、3.1.1 和 3.1 版本。它还提供 C 和 C++ 客户端库，以及用于发布、订阅和管理代理的命令行工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ikey4u/zebkit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zebkit – Chromium as an embeddable library</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit——可嵌入的 Chromium 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows. It includes Blink, V8 with its JIT tiers, PDFium, FFmpeg, and other Chrome components, but omits the Chrome browser and Electron runtime. Applications can use it through C, C++, Rust, Python, Go, or JavaScript bindings.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。它包含 Blink、带有各级 JIT 的 V8、PDFium、FFmpeg 及其他 Chrome 组件，但不包含 Chrome 浏览器本身或 Electron 运行时。应用可以通过 C、C++、Rust、Python、Go 或 JavaScript 绑定使用它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>109</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50468944" data-id="50468944" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|AI|Web|Code" data-score="107" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468944" data-time="1794497152">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chocoford/ExcalidrawZ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ: Local Native Excalidraw on macOS/iOS</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ：macOS/iOS 原生本地版 Excalidraw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas. It adds native document management, iCloud sync, history, export, AI assistance, MCP integration, and other Apple-platform features. Drawings are handled as application documents rather than loose browser downloads.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。它增加了原生文件管理、iCloud 同步、历史记录、导出、AI 辅助、MCP 集成及其他 Apple 平台功能。绘图会作为应用文档管理，而不是浏览器中零散下载的文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors. Its small character-level neural network was trained to generate Linux boot messages indefinitely, matching a simulator character by character. Debugging uncovered an x86 assembly bug involving the remainder left in EDX by integer division and a pointer that also used EDX.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。这个小型字符级神经网络经过训练，可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息，并与模拟器逐字符匹配。调试过程中发现了一个 x86 汇编错误：整数除法会把余数留在 EDX，而偏置表指针也使用了 EDX。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>105</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50444761" data-id="50444761" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Data|Code" data-score="102" data-comments="51" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50444761" data-time="1794337673">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0xdeadf1sh/T1DMAI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I have trained 15 blood glucose forecasting models on my own data</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我用自己的数据训练了 15 个血糖预测模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a model zoo for forecasting blood glucose levels, trained and tested on the author’s personal data as well as the public Metabonet dataset. It includes 15 forecasting models, including models developed by the author. Most of the repository’s code was written with AI assistance.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。集合中包含 15 个预测模型，其中也有作者自行开发的模型。仓库中的大部分代码由 AI 辅助编写。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。</span></p>

It generates assembly with a compiler installed locally, so it does not require an internet connection.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。它使用本地安装的编译器生成汇编代码，因此无需互联网连接。</span></p>
