<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ages2001/WINUDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Winudf – DVD / BluRay driver for DOS, Windows 1.x – 3x / 95 / 98 / ME</span><span class='zh'>Winudf：适用于 DOS、Windows 1.x–3.x、95、98、ME 的 DVD/Blu-ray 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WINUDF is an installable file-system driver that lets DOS and early Windows systems read and write UDF media. It supports UDF versions 1.02 through 2.60, including DVDs, Blu-ray discs, packet-written CDs, and UDF-formatted USB drives. DOS and Windows 1.0x–3.11 receive drive letters through WINUDF.EXE, while WINUDF.VXD provides a long-name file-system driver for Windows 95, 98, and ME.</span><span class='zh'>WINUDF 是一种可安装文件系统驱动，让 DOS 和早期 Windows 系统能够读写 UDF 媒体。它支持 1.02 至 2.60 版 UDF，涵盖 DVD、蓝光光盘、包写入 CD 以及采用 UDF 格式的 USB 驱动器。DOS 和 Windows 1.0x–3.11 可通过 WINUDF.EXE 获得盘符，Windows 95、98 和 ME 则使用支持长文件名的 WINUDF.VXD 驱动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/16/top_stories_11162026'><span class='en'>Nov 16</span><span class='zh'>11月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>95</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50515182' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50289590" data-id="50289590" data-tags="AI|Data|Science|Code" data-score="90" data-comments="10" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289590" data-time="1793283773">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Keplearn: Extract Equations from Data in Seconds</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn：秒级从数据中提取方程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems. This accelerates scientific experimentation and can complement traditional regression with interpretable models.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。这加速了科学实验，并可与传统回归方法结合，产生可解释的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>90</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289590' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50497147" data-id="50497147" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Hardware|Code" data-score="87" data-comments="26" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147" data-time="1794665292">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agweb – a browser for agents that stays out of your way</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Agweb——不妨碍用户操作的代理浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus. Agents control the single window from the shell through an agweb command modeled on Playwright CLI, while users can watch all tabs live in one view. Idle tabs are suspended; the author reports that five open pages used 0% CPU on a Mac, compared with five to seven busy cores from Chrome’s GPU process.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。代理通过类似 Playwright CLI 的 agweb 命令从 shell 控制这个窗口，用户则可以在一个视图中实时查看所有标签页。闲置标签页会被挂起；作者称，在 Mac 上打开五个页面时其 CPU 占用为 0%，而 Chrome 的 GPU 进程会占用五到七个繁忙核心。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>87</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497147' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>26</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50596664" data-id="50596664" data-tags="Show HN|Science|Education|Code" data-score="85" data-comments="58" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50596664" data-time="1795326390">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/tamnd/riemann-handbook" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tamnd/riemann-handbook" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: The Riemann Proof Handbook</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：《黎曼证明手册》</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Riemann Handbook presents the Lean proof of the Riemann Hypothesis from the tamnd/riemann-lean project in mathematical prose, with definitions, theorems, lemmas, and proofs.</span><span class='zh'>《黎曼证明手册》将 tamnd/riemann-lean 项目中的黎曼猜想 Lean 证明，以包含定义、定理、引理和证明的数学文字形式呈现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Riemann Handbook presents the Lean proof of the Riemann Hypothesis from the tamnd/riemann-lean project in mathematical prose, with definitions, theorems, lemmas, and proofs. It is available as a PDF and EPUB book. The claim has not been checked: the full Lean project has not been built, and the written statements have not been verified against the code.</span><span class='zh'>《黎曼证明手册》将 tamnd/riemann-lean 项目中的黎曼猜想 Lean 证明，以包含定义、定理、引理和证明的数学文字形式呈现。该书提供 PDF 和 EPUB 版本。但其中的数学主张尚未经过验证：完整 Lean 项目还没有构建成功，书面陈述也未与代码逐一核对。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>85</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50596664' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>58</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50460263" data-id="50460263" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="82" data-comments="14" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263" data-time="1794438567">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1195</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jjba23/sift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sift: LSP, Lint, Format Lisp, Scheme and Emacs Lisp</span><span class='zh'>Sift：用于 Lisp、Scheme 和 Emacs Lisp 的 LSP、Lint 与格式化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp. It reports malformed Lisp source and rewrites whitespace while preserving comments and read-time forms in their original positions. Formatting an already formatted file produces no further changes, a property asserted across the test suite.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。它会报告格式错误的 Lisp 源码并重写空白，同时保留注释和读取时形式的原始位置。对已经格式化的文件再次格式化不会产生变化，该特性在整个测试套件中都有断言验证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AGGIB/Stroq" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Stroq – a local audit of what your coding agent did and why</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Stroq——审计编程代理做了什么以及为何这样做</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stroq is an open-source local tool for inspecting Claude Code, Codex, and Cursor sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Stroq 是一款开源本地工具，用于检查 Claude Code、Codex 和 Cursor 的会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stroq is an open-source local tool for inspecting Claude Code, Codex, and Cursor sessions. It identifies known secrets, counts commands derived from text an agent read, and shows which keys left the machine. Local hooks can then trace risky commands back to the content that prompted them, without telemetry.</span><span class='zh'>Stroq 是一款开源本地工具，用于检查 Claude Code、Codex 和 Cursor 的会话。它可以识别已知密钥，统计代理从所读取文本中产生的命令，并显示哪些密钥离开了本机。用户还可以通过本地钩子将高风险命令追溯到触发它们的内容，而且不会发送遥测数据。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Moxnox 1.0, an NT-inspired, POSIX-compatible OS built on the Btrfs filesystem, signaling an experimental approach to combining Windows-like design with modern filesystems.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Moxnox 1.0，这是一个在 Btrfs 文件系统上实现的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容的操作系统原型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Moxnox 1.0, an NT-inspired, POSIX-compatible OS built on the Btrfs filesystem, signaling an experimental approach to combining Windows-like design with modern filesystems.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Moxnox 1.0，这是一个在 Btrfs 文件系统上实现的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容的操作系统原型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/piercefreeman/waymark" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Waymark – A workflow engine that compiles jobs instead of replaying</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Waymark——编译任务而非重放任务的工作流引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS. It lets developers write workflows in ordinary Python code using conditionals, loops, and asyncio-style parallelism, while the runtime handles distributed execution. Rather than replaying past state in an interpreter, Waymark walks the program’s AST and compiles the logic into bytecode for a stackless virtual machine.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。开发者可以用普通 Python 代码编写工作流，包括条件语句、循环和类似 asyncio 的并行操作，再由运行时负责分布式执行。Waymark 不通过解释器重放历史状态，而是遍历程序 AST，将逻辑编译为运行在无栈虚拟机上的字节码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>81</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379221' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50519778" data-id="50519778" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Data|Code" data-score="80" data-comments="22" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50519778" data-time="1794848203">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/theconsensuslabs/noalloc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>noalloc: Tooling and libraries to enforce zero heap allocations in Golang</span><span class='zh'>noalloc：在 Golang 中实现零堆分配的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services. It helps developers reason about memory use, though it may constrain certain idioms and require careful design.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。它帮助开发者管理内存使用，但可能限制某些编码习惯并需谨慎设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shikokuchuo/pymizu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mizu – zero-copy parallel R and Python over shared memory</span><span class='zh'>Mizu：通过共享内存实现 R 与 Python 的零拷贝并行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory. Its benchmarks report reducing task round trips from roughly 100 microseconds over sockets to less than 1 microsecond. Lists, dictionaries, NumPy arrays, and Arrow tables can be shared between Python and R as zero-copy views.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。其基准测试称，任务往返时间可从基于套接字的约 100 微秒降至不到 1 微秒。列表、字典、NumPy 数组和 Arrow 表都可以作为零拷贝视图在 Python 与 R 之间共享。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>72</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50380051' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>11</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50278678" data-id="50278678" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Politics|Code" data-score="71" data-comments="13" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50278678" data-time="1793212253">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/emmilco/us-tax-model" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/emmilco/us-tax-model" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A proof-of-concept formalization of the IRS federal tax forms</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN project formalizes IRS tax forms as a proof-of-concept, likely using a theorem prover or formal spec; demonstrates how to model tax forms for correctness and tooling; significance for software that handles regulated forms.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现，借助形式化工具提高正确性与可验证性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN project formalizes IRS tax forms as a proof-of-concept, likely using a theorem prover or formal spec; demonstrates how to model tax forms for correctness and tooling; significance for software that handles regulated forms. 2-3 sentences.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现，借助形式化工具提高正确性与可验证性。此举对政府表单数字化和软件可靠性具有示范价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/popvax/yash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cancer vaccine for Yash, he&#x27;s diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer</span><span class='zh'>Yash 的癌症疫苗：他被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry. Yashdeep Bindal was diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer in January 2025 at age 32, shortly after returning to work following the birth of his daughter Maya.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。Yashdeep Bindal 于 2025 年 1 月、32 岁时被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌；此前他刚在女儿 Maya 出生后重返工作岗位。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FURSOY/fursoy-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Fursoy – Out-of-order RV64IMAFDV core in SystemVerilog (Linux 6.6)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 SystemVerilog 编写、支持乱序执行的 RV64IMAFDV 核心（Linux 6.6）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fursoy is a 64-bit out-of-order superscalar RISC-V processor implemented in SystemVerilog. It supports RV64IMAFDV with Zba and Zbb extensions, boots mainline Linux 6.6.50 and BusyBox under Sv39 virtual memory, and includes separate machine, supervisor, and user privilege levels. Synthesis in Nangate 45nm reached 500 MHz timing closure across eight core functional blocks.</span><span class='zh'>Fursoy 是一款用 SystemVerilog 实现的 64 位乱序超标量 RISC-V 处理器。它支持带 Zba 和 Zbb 扩展的 RV64IMAFDV，可在 Sv39 虚拟内存下启动主线 Linux 6.6.50 和 BusyBox，并具备机器、监管者和用户三级权限。该设计在 Nangate 45nm 工艺中完成了八个核心功能模块的 500 MHz 时序收敛。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/14/top_stories_11142026'><span class='en'>Nov 14</span><span class='zh'>11月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>70</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50497137' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>23</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50454951" data-id="50454951" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="69" data-comments="29" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454951" data-time="1794412850">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/klad-app/klad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Klad, a paint app your coding agent can drive (source-available)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Klad，一款可由编码代理驱动的绘画应用（源代码可用）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans. It supports brush painting, frame-by-frame animation, bones and inverse kinematics, vectors, text layers, colors, layer effects, and persistent unlimited undo. Its editable strokes are intended to let agents create drafts that people can refine stroke by stroke.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。它支持笔刷绘画、逐帧动画、骨骼与反向运动学、矢量、文本图层、颜色、图层效果，以及可跨文件重新打开保留的无限撤销。它将可编辑笔触作为核心，使代理能够生成草稿，再由人类逐笔修改。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>69</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454951' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>29</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50486914" data-id="50486914" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="69" data-comments="17" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486914" data-time="1794591543">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/basetenlabs/deepseek-ultrafast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Ultrafast is an inference engine for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash targeting NVIDIA sm_100a and sm_103a hardware. It supports a 1-million-token context length and multi-server KV-cache sharing. The project measures decode speed, step time, and acceptance length using a Tetris-generation prompt.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Ultrafast 是面向 NVIDIA sm_100a 和 sm_103a 硬件的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 推理引擎。它支持 100 万 token 的上下文长度和多服务器 KV 缓存共享。项目使用生成俄罗斯方块的提示词测量解码速度、步骤耗时和接受长度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><span class='en'>Nov 13</span><span class='zh'>11月13日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>69</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50486914' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>17</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50405263" data-id="50405263" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="64" data-comments="12" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405263" data-time="1794064561">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Video-Rebirth/hyperflow-h3-flash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: We compressed a 31B video+audio diffusion model to 14B, 6 steps, INT8</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将 31B 视频音频扩散模型压缩至 14B、6 步、INT8</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters. It generates video and synchronized stereo audio together and supports INT8 and GGUF formats. The project also provides weights, ComfyUI nodes, a GGUF setup guide, and an eight-step LoRA for the full-size model.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。它能够同时生成视频和同步立体声，并支持 INT8 与 GGUF 格式。项目还提供模型权重、ComfyUI 节点、GGUF 配置指南，以及适用于完整模型的 8 步 LoRA。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alesha-pro/llm.asm/tree/main/02-baremetal-os" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bare Metal Qwen</span><span class='zh'>裸机 Qwen</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bootable x86-64 operating system runs Qwen3-0.6B inference directly on hardware or KVM using pure NASM assembly.</span><span class='zh'>一个可启动的 x86-64 操作系统使用纯 NASM 汇编，直接在硬件或 KVM 上运行 Qwen3-0.6B 推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bootable x86-64 operating system runs Qwen3-0.6B inference directly on hardware or KVM using pure NASM assembly. The project includes a 12 KB inference binary, a 22 KB bare-metal OS, and a 32 KB Mini-GPT trainer with a live Braille interface. It uses no Linux, Windows, C, Rust, libc, or other dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>一个可启动的 x86-64 操作系统使用纯 NASM 汇编，直接在硬件或 KVM 上运行 Qwen3-0.6B 推理。项目包含 12 KB 的推理程序、22 KB 的裸机操作系统，以及带实时盲文界面的 32 KB Mini-GPT 训练器。不依赖 Linux、Windows、C、Rust、libc 或其他依赖项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Apakottur/unref" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Unref – A Python dead code finder built on Ruff&#x27;s parser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Unref——基于 Ruff 解析器的 Python 死代码查找器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unref is a Python dead-code finder built with Rust and Ruff&#x27;s parser, created because existing alternatives did not work reliably on the author&#x27;s codebases. It can scan all of CPython in under a second and has found substantial dead code in the author&#x27;s team&#x27;s projects. The tool is intended for low-configuration use and is already being used in continuous integration.</span><span class='zh'>Unref 是一款使用 Rust 和 Ruff 解析器构建的 Python 死代码查找器，起因是现有替代工具无法可靠适配作者的代码库。它能在不到一秒内检查完整的 CPython，并在作者团队的项目中发现了大量死代码。该工具定位于低配置使用，目前已用于持续集成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyphatech/spindle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Spindle – a full-featured, type-safe web framework for OCaml 5</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Spindle，面向 OCaml 5 的全功能类型安全 Web 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas. It includes an RFC-strict HTTP server, WebSockets, SSE, MCP server primitives, tracing, metrics, and logging. Its benchmarks report performance two times faster than NestJS and five times faster than FastAPI on a single core, although speed was not the project’s primary goal.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。它包含严格遵循 RFC 的 HTTP 服务器、WebSocket、SSE、MCP 服务器基础组件，以及分布式追踪、指标和日志功能。基准测试显示，它在单核上运行速度约为 NestJS 的两倍、FastAPI 的五倍，不过速度并非项目的首要目标。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/CeleritasCelery/rune" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rune: Rust VM for Emacs</span><span class='zh'>Rune：用于 Emacs 的 Rust 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rune is an experimental Emacs core written in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个用 Rust 编写的实验性 Emacs 核心。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rune is an experimental Emacs core written in Rust. Its current goal is an editor MVP with a basic Emacs Lisp runtime and basic editing functionality in a minimal GUI. The project remains at a very early stage.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个用 Rust 编写的实验性 Emacs 核心。当前目标是实现编辑器最小可行版本，包括基础 Emacs Lisp 运行时，以及运行在极简图形界面中的基本编辑功能。项目目前仍处于非常早期的阶段。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tom7/noperts/blob/main/septopert.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smallest possible nopert polyhedra: the Septopert</span><span class='zh'>最小的非鲁珀特多面体：Septopert</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Septopert is a convex seven-vertex polyhedron that is not Rupert, meaning it cannot pass through a congruent copy of itself. Seven vertices is the minimum possible for a non-Rupert polyhedron. The page gives exact statements, Lean-checked proofs, and reproduction instructions for the canonical Septopert.</span><span class='zh'>Septopert 是一种拥有 7 个顶点、且不是鲁珀特多面体的凸多面体，也就是说它无法穿过与自身全等的另一个多面体。7 个顶点是非鲁珀特多面体可能达到的最少数量。页面提供了规范 Septopert 的精确陈述、经 Lean 检查的证明和复现步骤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/XITRIX/ppsspp-nx2/blob/master/SWITCH2-GAMESHARE.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running two PSP emulators on one Switch 2 and sharing one via GameShare</span><span class='zh'>在一台 Switch 2 上运行两个 PSP 模拟器，并通过 GameShare 共享一个</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A branch of the PPSSPP emulator for Switch 2 experiments with running a separate PSP guest on the host console and streaming it to a stock GameShare client.</span><span class='zh'>Switch 2 版 PPSSPP 模拟器的一个分支尝试在主机上运行独立的 PSP 客体，并将其串流到普通 GameShare 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A branch of the PPSSPP emulator for Switch 2 experiments with running a separate PSP guest on the host console and streaming it to a stock GameShare client. The setup is not a mirrored controller or a second PSP controller in the host emulator. Compilation and host tests have not yet established console operation or acceptable two-game performance.</span><span class='zh'>Switch 2 版 PPSSPP 模拟器的一个分支尝试在主机上运行独立的 PSP 客体，并将其串流到普通 GameShare 客户端。该方案并不是镜像控制器，也不是在主模拟器中增加第二个 PSP 控制器。编译和主机测试尚未证明它能在游戏机上运行，也未证明双游戏性能达到可接受水平。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/crmne/zapfast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZapFast: A fast, native WhatsApp client in Rust and egui</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast：使用 Rust 和 egui 构建的快速原生 WhatsApp 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZapFast is a lightweight, native WhatsApp client written in Rust with an egui interface.</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast 是一款使用 Rust 编写、基于 egui 界面的轻量级原生 WhatsApp 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZapFast is a lightweight, native WhatsApp client written in Rust with an egui interface. It is designed to run on Linux, macOS, and Windows. The project provides downloads, setup instructions, a user guide, and contribution information.</span><span class='zh'>ZapFast 是一款使用 Rust 编写、基于 egui 界面的轻量级原生 WhatsApp 客户端。它面向 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 平台。项目提供下载、安装说明、用户指南和参与贡献的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/Micron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Micron – Oberon with the Power of C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Micron——带有 C 能力的 Oberon</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability. Its goal is to use one language for boot code, device drivers, operating-system kernels, and ordinary applications without a garbage collector. It supports explicit memory layouts, signed and unsigned integers, pointer operations, and direct C interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。它的目标是用同一种语言编写启动代码、设备驱动、操作系统内核和普通应用，同时不依赖垃圾回收器。该语言支持显式内存布局、有符号和无符号整数、指针操作以及直接的 C 互操作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>53</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413266' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50541047" data-id="50541047" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="52" data-comments="21" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50541047" data-time="1794968309">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kborer/cells/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: An ASCII cell aquarium for your terminal</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：终端里的 ASCII 细胞水族箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This terminal program presents a virtual microscope slide rendered in ASCII characters.</span><span class='zh'>这个终端程序用 ASCII 字符呈现一个虚拟显微镜载玻片。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This terminal program presents a virtual microscope slide rendered in ASCII characters. Cells divide, immune cells chase bacteria, and bacteriophages occasionally create disruptions. It can be installed with pipx and run using the `cells` command.</span><span class='zh'>这个终端程序用 ASCII 字符呈现一个虚拟显微镜载玻片。细胞会分裂，免疫细胞会追逐细菌，噬菌体也会不时制造混乱。程序可通过 pipx 安装，并使用 `cells` 命令运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/arterialist/kube32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I rebuilt Kubernetes for ESP32 microcontrollers</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我为 ESP32 微控制器重做了 Kubernetes</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers. The project lets kubectl and k9s manage boards over Wi-Fi or USB serial, while pods run WebAssembly modules through the wasm3 interpreter. A board with PSRAM serves as the control plane, and boards without PSRAM act as workers. The same sources also build a macOS and Linux host simulator.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。该项目支持通过 Wi-Fi 或 USB 串口使用 kubectl 和 k9s 管理开发板，并通过 wasm3 解释器运行 WebAssembly 模块。带 PSRAM 的开发板充当控制平面，不带 PSRAM 的开发板作为工作节点。同一套源代码还可构建 macOS 和 Linux 主机模拟器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>50</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50291812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50394977" data-id="50394977" data-tags="Programming|Education|Code" data-score="49" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50394977" data-time="1793984380">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1475</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thesayfulla/pylings" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PyLings: Solve Exercises and Learn Python</span><span class='zh'>PyLings：通过练习学习 Python</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PyLings teaches Python by having users fix code in exercises ranging from basic concepts to senior-level interview problems.</span><span class='zh'>PyLings 通过让用户修复代码来学习 Python，练习范围从基础知识延伸到高级面试题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PyLings teaches Python by having users fix code in exercises ranging from basic concepts to senior-level interview problems. It includes 35 warm-ups and 20 interview exercises, requires no dependencies, and targets Python 3.14. The tool opens the next exercise and reruns its tests whenever the user saves.</span><span class='zh'>PyLings 通过让用户修复代码来学习 Python，练习范围从基础知识延伸到高级面试题。它包含 35 道热身题和 20 道面试题，不依赖额外软件包，面向 Python 3.14。用户每次保存时，工具都会打开下一道练习并重新运行测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pigeon-labs-stack/v100-sxm2-reference" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>V100 SXM2 pinout and NVLink reference</span><span class='zh'>V100 SXM2 引脚图与 NVLink 参考资料</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project collects V100 SXM2 pin maps, two- and four-GPU NVLink connection plans, and connector-only KiCad resources.</span><span class='zh'>该项目整理了 V100 SXM2 引脚图、双 GPU 和四 GPU 的 NVLink 连接方案，以及仅含连接器的 KiCad 资源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project collects V100 SXM2 pin maps, two- and four-GPU NVLink connection plans, and connector-only KiCad resources. It aims to support an open-source carrier-board design for Nvidia V100 SXM2 modules, while enabling others to develop their own designs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目整理了 V100 SXM2 引脚图、双 GPU 和四 GPU 的 NVLink 连接方案，以及仅含连接器的 KiCad 资源。项目目标是支持 Nvidia V100 SXM2 模块的开源载板设计，同时让其他人能够据此开发自己的设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graealex/WaveDudeNext" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext 2D Wave Simulator</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 二维波模拟器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics. Users can turn images into refractive scenes, place light sources and optical components, and observe refraction, diffraction, and interference. It displays both the wave field and time-averaged intensity, while supporting probes, timeline control, video export, adjustable brightness, custom color schemes, and multiple interface languages.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。用户可以将图像转换为折射场景，放置光源和光学元件，并观察折射、衍射与干涉现象。程序同时显示波场和时间平均强度，并支持探针、时间轴控制、视频导出、亮度调节、自定义配色以及多种界面语言。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1502</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Tthanh2k6/turbo-browser-mcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Turbo-Browser-MCP – Batch forms, DOM deltas and submit guards for MCP</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Turbo-Browser-MCP——为 MCP 提供批量表单、DOM 增量和提交保护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbo-Browser-MCP is a Playwright-based MCP server designed to reduce token usage and multi-turn latency in browser automation. It combines 25–33 fine-grained browser primitives into 10 batched tools, with delta DOM snapshots, React/Shadow DOM/iframe form filling, direct disk uploads, parallel tabs, and network-blocking presets. It also includes a default human-in-the-loop guard before form submission.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo-Browser-MCP 是一个基于 Playwright 的 MCP 服务器，旨在降低浏览器自动化中的 token 消耗和多轮交互延迟。它将 25 至 33 个细粒度浏览器原语整合为 10 个批量工具，并支持 DOM 增量快照、React/Shadow DOM/iframe 表单填写、直接从磁盘上传文件、并行标签页和网络阻断预设。提交表单前，它还默认提供人工介入保护。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/15/top_stories_11152026'><span class='en'>Nov 15</span><span class='zh'>11月15日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>47</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50503930' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>9</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50443264" data-id="50443264" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Data|DevOps|Code" data-score="46" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264" data-time="1794330969">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/OpenTSLM/TimeNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1293904978/25bd41c7-c628-42cb-9c24-01c18b0f3a31' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1505</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTSLM/TimeNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TimeNet: Open-source data infrastructure for Temporal Foundation Models</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet：面向时间基础模型的开源数据基础设施</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models. It supports tasks including classification, question answering, scalar prediction, temporal localization, forecasting, generation, editing, and correspondence. The project reports that training on multiple temporal datasets improved average F1 performance by 14% for OpenTSLM and 22% for SLIP.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。它支持分类、问答、标量预测、时间定位、预测、生成、编辑和对应关系等任务。项目方称，在多个时间数据集上训练后，OpenTSLM 的平均 F1 性能提升了 14%，SLIP 提升了 22%。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>46</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50533270" data-id="50533270" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="46" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50533270" data-time="1794931264">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/RunanywhereAI/trnrt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1440096236/8057fa03-91df-46f8-9d49-309f457392f0' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1508</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RunanywhereAI/trnrt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TrnRT – An open-source LLM inference engine for AWS Trainium</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TrnRT——面向 AWS Trainium 的开源 LLM 推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RunAnywhere open-sourced TrnRT, an inference engine for AWS Trainium that currently serves GLM-5.3 Flash in FP8 on an AWS trn2.48xlarge instance. It can serve two model instances on one box using eight Trainium2 chips and a cache-aware router. On the SemiAnalysis AgentX benchmark, the team reports 3.57 million tokens per minute. The project targets large models with long context windows and aims to improve cost efficiency compared with H100 and H200 deployments.</span><span class='zh'>RunAnywhere 开源了 TrnRT，这是一个面向 AWS Trainium 的推理引擎，目前可在 AWS trn2.48xlarge 实例上以 FP8 运行 GLM-5.3 Flash。它可借助 8 个 Trainium2 芯片和感知缓存的路由器，在一台机器上服务两个模型实例。团队称，其在 SemiAnalysis AgentX 基准上的性能达到每分钟 357 万个 token。该项目面向长上下文大模型，目标是相较 H100 和 H200 部署提升成本效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/17/top_stories_11172026'><span class='en'>Nov 17</span><span class='zh'>11月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>46</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50533270' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>5</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50415078" data-id="50415078" data-tags="Security|Open Source|Education|Code" data-score="43" data-comments="12" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415078" data-time="1794157795">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/vsmutok/FuckCaptcha" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1532</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vsmutok/FuckCaptcha" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A field guide to CAPTCHA types and how they break</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 类型及其破解方式实地指南</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives. It provides a searchable web version and frames the material for education and authorized research. Its stated goal is to make changing CAPTCHA systems easier to navigate, compare, and cite while emphasizing responsible use.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。项目将材料定位为教育和获授权研究用途。其目标是在强调负责任使用的同时，让不断变化的 CAPTCHA 系统更易于查找、比较和引用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>43</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415078' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>12</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50302686" data-id="50302686" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="43" data-comments="21" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50302686" data-time="1793361024">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/skorotkiewicz/c59e591c26661cb52b06afc42d8ac546" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decode kernel panic from QR Code photo</span><span class='zh'>从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is titled “decode kernel panic from QR Code photo.” It is presented as a GitHub Gist, but the available page content does not provide the decoding method or a working example.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目名为“从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息”。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is titled “decode kernel panic from QR Code photo.” It is presented as a GitHub Gist, but the available page content does not provide the decoding method or a working example.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目名为“从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息”。它以 GitHub Gist 的形式呈现，但现有页面内容没有提供解码方法或可运行示例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/plabayo/venndb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: VennDB: append-only in-memory Rust DB for rows queried using bit flags</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：VennDB——用位标志查询行数据的追加式内存 Rust 数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VennDB is an append-only, in-memory database written in Rust that queries rows through bit-flag columns. It targets mostly static datasets loaded at startup and queried frequently with simple filters, with an emphasis on speed and compactness. For its limited use cases, the project has few dependencies and is described as faster than a naive implementation or a heavier option such as SQLite.</span><span class='zh'>VennDB 是一个用 Rust 编写的追加式内存数据库，通过位标志列查询行数据。它面向主要在启动时加载、之后频繁使用简单过滤条件查询的静态数据集，重点是速度和紧凑性。在适用范围有限的场景中，该项目依赖很少，并被描述为比朴素实现或 SQLite 等更重量级方案更快。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/peterretief/codemap" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Codemap – one-page interactive map of any Go repository</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Codemap——用一页交互式地图展示任意 Go 仓库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codemap generates a single HTML page mapping a Go module’s packages, dependencies, files, and functions.</span><span class='zh'>Codemap 会生成一个 HTML 页面，展示 Go 模块中的包、依赖、文件和函数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codemap generates a single HTML page mapping a Go module’s packages, dependencies, files, and functions. Users can search the map, inspect what each package uses and what uses it, and jump to function definitions in GitHub-hosted modules. Tests are excluded from the map.</span><span class='zh'>Codemap 会生成一个 HTML 页面，展示 Go 模块中的包、依赖、文件和函数。用户可以搜索地图，查看每个包依赖什么以及被什么依赖，并跳转到 GitHub 模块中的函数定义。地图不包含测试代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/n4ze3m/tiny-voca" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 4.5M-parameter TTS model runs 70x real-time on CPU and cost just $10 to train</span><span class='zh'>一款 450 万参数的 TTS 模型在 CPU 上以实时速度 70 倍运行，训练成本仅 10 美元</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tiny-voca is a small English text-to-speech model with two voices, F2 and M2. Its complete model, including the waveform generator, has 4.5 million parameters and was trained from scratch on about 18 hours of synthetic speech for roughly $10. On four CPU threads, it runs at about 70 times real time.</span><span class='zh'>Tiny-voca 是一款支持 F2 和 M2 两种声音的轻量级英语文本转语音模型。包含波形生成器在内的完整模型有 450 万个参数，使用约 18 小时合成语音从头训练，成本约为 10 美元。在四个 CPU 线程上，它的运行速度约为实时速度的 70 倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Dodaios/ste80-humanizer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: STE80 Humanizer – de-AI text by writing 80% of the way to ASD-STE100</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer：将 AI 文本改写至接近 ASD-STE100 的 80%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning. It targets roughly 80% compliance with ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English. The example focuses on Kubernetes liveness probes and retains both their restart behavior and the warning that misconfiguration can cause cascading restarts.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。它的目标是让文本达到约 80% 的 ASD-STE100 简明技术英语规范。示例内容围绕 Kubernetes 存活探针展开，并保留了探针触发重启的行为以及配置错误可能导致级联重启的警告。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/matthewmrichter/mooftcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MoofTCP – A modern replacement for MacTCP on 68k Macs</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP——68K Mac 上 MacTCP 的现代替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoofTCP is a drop-in replacement for Apple&#x27;s MacTCP on System 6 and 68000-based Macs.</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP 是 System 6 和 68000 Mac 上苹果 MacTCP 的即插即用替代品。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoofTCP is a drop-in replacement for Apple&#x27;s MacTCP on System 6 and 68000-based Macs. Existing MacTCP programs can run unchanged on its newly written, portable-C TCP/IP stack. It includes its own control panel, installer, and DHCP client, and was designed for a Mac Classic with 4 MB of RAM.</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP 是 System 6 和 68000 Mac 上苹果 MacTCP 的即插即用替代品。现有的 MacTCP 程序无需修改即可运行在其用可移植 C 语言重写的 TCP/IP 协议栈上。它还包含独立的控制面板、安装程序和 DHCP 客户端，并针对仅有 4 MB 内存的 Mac Classic 设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aleff-github/onion-routing-anatomy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anatomia Di Tor – The Tor Protocol, Byte by Byte</span><span class='zh'>Anatomia Di Tor：逐字节解析 Tor 协议</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anatomia Di Tor is a free, open-source technical book that explores the Tor protocol byte by byte.</span><span class='zh'>《Anatomia Di Tor》是一本免费开源的技术书，逐字节讲解 Tor 协议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anatomia Di Tor is a free, open-source technical book that explores the Tor protocol byte by byte. It includes 20 reproducible laboratories running on a private Tor network, documentation, and multilingual editions. The project is released under the GPL-3.0-or-later license.</span><span class='zh'>《Anatomia Di Tor》是一本免费开源的技术书，逐字节讲解 Tor 协议。它包含在私有 Tor 网络上运行的 20 个可复现实验、文档以及多语言版本。项目采用 GPL-3.0 或更高版本许可证发布。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/no382001/zipc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A ZIP-style Prolog VM for trilog</span><span class='zh'>用于 trilog 的 ZIP 风格 Prolog 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C. Its goal is to provide enough of a VM to compare it with the current trilog runtime. ZIP is the abstract machine described in the 1984 book Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。其目标是先搭建足够完整的虚拟机，以便与当前的 trilog 运行时进行比较。ZIP 是 1984 年《Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine》中描述的抽象机器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lonthq/overtone-captions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Overtone – Transcribe, correct and cut captions for any screen shape</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Overtone — 适用于任意屏幕形状的字幕转写、纠错与裁剪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Overtone is a show-n-HN project that transcribes, corrects, and cuts captions to fit any screen shape.</span><span class='zh'>Overtone 是 Show HN 项目，能够转写、纠错并将字幕裁剪以适配任意屏幕形状，提升无障碍和本地化工作效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Overtone is a show-n-HN project that transcribes, corrects, and cuts captions to fit any screen shape. It advances accessibility and localization workflows by automating caption adaptation. The tool can help creators reach diverse audiences across devices with fewer manual tweaks.</span><span class='zh'>Overtone 是 Show HN 项目，能够转写、纠错并将字幕裁剪以适配任意屏幕形状，提升无障碍和本地化工作效率。它帮助内容创作者覆盖多设备场景，省去大量手动调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PQCrypta/pqcrypta-proxy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PQCrypta Proxy: Rust proxy with HTTP/3, QUIC, hybrid post-quantum TLS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：PQCrypta Proxy——支持 HTTP/3、QUIC 和混合后量子 TLS 的 Rust 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PQCrypta Proxy is a Rust Layer-7 reverse proxy supporting HTTP/1.1, HTTP/2, HTTP/3/QUIC, and WebTransport.</span><span class='zh'>PQCrypta Proxy 是一个用 Rust 编写的七层反向代理，支持 HTTP/1.1、HTTP/2、HTTP/3/QUIC 和 WebTransport。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PQCrypta Proxy is a Rust Layer-7 reverse proxy supporting HTTP/1.1, HTTP/2, HTTP/3/QUIC, and WebTransport. It includes hybrid post-quantum TLS using X25519MLKEM768, along with fingerprinting, GeoIP blocking, circuit breaking, adaptive rate limiting, load balancing, compression, and observability features. The project also supports ACME, OCSP stapling, and hot reloading.</span><span class='zh'>PQCrypta Proxy 是一个用 Rust 编写的七层反向代理，支持 HTTP/1.1、HTTP/2、HTTP/3/QUIC 和 WebTransport。它提供基于 X25519MLKEM768 的混合后量子 TLS，以及指纹识别、GeoIP 封锁、熔断、自适应限流、负载均衡、压缩和监控功能。项目还支持 ACME、OCSP 装订和热加载。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/24/top_stories_11242026'><span class='en'>Nov 24</span><span class='zh'>11月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>33</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50591024' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50506940" data-id="50506940" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI|Data|Code" data-score="33" data-comments="10" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50506940" data-time="1794750651">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/db-branch/db-branch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: db-branch – database branches in seconds for AI agents and CI, via ZFS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：db-branch——通过 ZFS 为 AI 代理和 CI 秒速创建数据库分支</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>db-branch creates copy-on-write branches of large MySQL and PostgreSQL databases in seconds using ZFS clones. The self-hosted tool can provide isolated databases for migrations, AI agents, or pull requests without copying the underlying data. Branches can be reset or discarded, and the project is licensed under Apache-2.0.</span><span class='zh'>db-branch 使用 ZFS 克隆，在几秒内为大型 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 数据库创建写时复制分支。这款自托管工具无需复制底层数据，就能为迁移、AI 代理或每个拉取请求提供独立数据库。分支可以重置或丢弃，项目采用 Apache-2.0 许可证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vapmuz/Mocktastic" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mocktastic: A Meshtastic Node on Arduino</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic：Arduino 上的 Meshtastic 节点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mocktastic is a small, customizable Meshtastic-compatible LoRa client for ESP32 boards built with ArduinoIDE.</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic 是一个面向 ESP32 开发板、使用 ArduinoIDE 构建的小型可定制 Meshtastic 兼容 LoRa 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mocktastic is a small, customizable Meshtastic-compatible LoRa client for ESP32 boards built with ArduinoIDE. It is an educational and experimental C++ implementation rather than a fork of the official firmware. The project demonstrates Meshtastic packet encoding and decoding and includes an environmental-sensor example.</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic 是一个面向 ESP32 开发板、使用 ArduinoIDE 构建的小型可定制 Meshtastic 兼容 LoRa 客户端。它是用于教育和实验的 C++ 实现，并非官方固件的分支。项目演示了 Meshtastic 数据包的编码与解码，并包含环境传感器示例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Millillion/lasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Lasm – Write formally verified webapps in Lean for Node/serverless/edge</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Lasm——用 Lean 为 Node/无服务器/边缘计算编写形式化验证的网页应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lasm lets developers write web applications in Lean with formal verification, targeting Node, serverless, and edge environments.</span><span class='zh'>Lasm 让开发者用 Lean 编写并进行形式化验证的网络应用，面向 Node、无服务器/边缘计算环境。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lasm lets developers write web applications in Lean with formal verification, targeting Node, serverless, and edge environments. It demonstrates how strong guarantees can be baked into modern web apps, reducing runtime errors and increasing security.</span><span class='zh'>Lasm 让开发者用 Lean 编写并进行形式化验证的网络应用，面向 Node、无服务器/边缘计算环境。展示了在现代 Web 开发中嵌入定理证明保证的可行性与潜在收益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Christ10-8/reflota" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Reflota – Open-source data recovery for NTFS/FAT disks with phased copy</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Reflota——支持分阶段复制的 NTFS/FAT 开源数据恢复工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflota is a free, open-source, read-only tool for recovering files from NTFS, FAT32, FAT16, FAT12, and exFAT disks, including deleted files. It reads disks directly: for NTFS, it reconstructs the folder tree from the master file table and directory metadata, while for FAT and exFAT it reads directories and follows cluster chains. It is intended for cases where Windows reports that a disk is unformatted, a folder will not open, or Explorer hangs.</span><span class='zh'>Reflota 是一款免费、开源且只读的数据恢复工具，支持从 NTFS、FAT32、FAT16、FAT12 和 exFAT 磁盘中恢复文件，包括已删除的文件。它会直接读取磁盘：处理 NTFS 时读取主文件表并根据目录元数据重建文件夹树，处理 FAT 和 exFAT 时读取目录并跟踪簇链。当 Windows 报告磁盘未格式化、文件夹无法打开或资源管理器卡死时，该工具可用于尝试恢复数据。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gauge-sh/gauge-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gauge – Open-Source CLI to recursively improve your docs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Gauge——递归改进文档的开源 CLI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gauge is an open-source CLI that recursively improves documentation for coding agents.</span><span class='zh'>Gauge 是一个用于递归改进 coding agent 文档的开源 CLI。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gauge is an open-source CLI that recursively improves documentation for coding agents. It runs tasks, analyzes agent transcripts for friction or failures, creates documentation variants, and repeats the process until a variant resolves the issues. The tool includes a local execution backend and can also optimize skills, CLIs, and other agent-facing interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Gauge 是一个用于递归改进 coding agent 文档的开源 CLI。它会运行任务、分析 agent 会话记录中的阻碍或失败、生成文档变体，并重复这一过程，直到某个变体解决相关问题。该工具包含本地执行后端，也可用于优化技能、CLI 和其他面向 agent 的界面。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>29</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371090' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>4</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50525052" data-id="50525052" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="29" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50525052" data-time="1794871541">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0nelight/sutra-os" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sutra-OS – A self-hosted ontology platform</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Sutra-OS——自托管本体平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform.</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sutra-OS is an open-source, self-hosted ontology platform. It represents data as one owned model with typed and linked entities, server-enforced rules, and numbers traceable to their sources. The project describes its design as “Scarce Units, Typed, Related, Actionable.”</span><span class='zh'>Sutra-OS 是一个开源、自托管的本体平台。它将数据表示为用户拥有的统一模型，其中实体具有类型和关联，修改由服务器规则强制执行，所有数字都能追溯到来源。项目将其设计概括为“稀缺、类型化、关联、可行动”。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/smokeeaasd/flawless-ui-ux/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flawless UI-UX Skill 1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>Flawless UI-UX Skill 1.0.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flawless UI-UX is a skill for coding agents that helps them build distinctive and usable interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Flawless UI-UX 是一项帮助编程智能体构建具有特色且易用界面的技能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flawless UI-UX is a skill for coding agents that helps them build distinctive and usable interfaces. Version 1.0.0 includes an interactive installer and support for twelve agents. The installer can be run with npm, pnpm, or Bun, and lets users choose project or global scope, select agents, review destinations, and confirm file copying.</span><span class='zh'>Flawless UI-UX 是一项帮助编程智能体构建具有特色且易用界面的技能。1.0.0 版本提供交互式安装程序，并支持 12 种智能体。用户可以通过 npm、pnpm 或 Bun 启动安装器，选择项目级或全局范围、指定智能体、检查目标位置并确认复制文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmpereComputing/panthera" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Panthera: Ampere&#x27;s CPU Performance Model</span><span class='zh'>Panthera：Ampere 的 CPU 性能模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Panthera is Ampere’s cycle-accurate software performance model for studying prospective CPU features and evaluating microarchitectural trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>Panthera 是 Ampere 的周期精确软件性能模型，用于研究候选 CPU 特性并评估微架构取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Panthera is Ampere’s cycle-accurate software performance model for studying prospective CPU features and evaluating microarchitectural trade-offs. It is built by Ampere’s Architecture &amp; Performance team to guide development of high-performance cloud processors. The project includes a user guide for building and using Panthera and its associated tools.</span><span class='zh'>Panthera 是 Ampere 的周期精确软件性能模型，用于研究候选 CPU 特性并评估微架构取舍。它由 Ampere 架构与性能团队开发，以指导高性能云处理器的设计。项目还提供了构建和使用 Panthera 及相关工具的用户指南。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hatchet-dev/brickie" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Brickie – Deploy sandboxes in your own AWS account</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Brickie——在自己的 AWS 账户中部署沙箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Brickie is an open-source project for running sandboxes in a user’s own cloud account, primarily on AWS EKS.</span><span class='zh'>Brickie 是一个开源项目，允许用户在自己的云账户中运行沙箱，主要面向 AWS EKS。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Brickie is an open-source project for running sandboxes in a user’s own cloud account, primarily on AWS EKS. The sandboxes let agents execute untrusted code and preserve state through filesystem snapshots, using isolation layers such as Firecracker, gVisor, and Kata Containers. The project targets organizations that need to self-host sandbox infrastructure for enterprise workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Brickie 是一个开源项目，允许用户在自己的云账户中运行沙箱，主要面向 AWS EKS。沙箱可让代理执行不可信代码，并通过文件系统快照保存状态，支持 Firecracker、gVisor 和 Kata Containers 等隔离层。该项目面向因企业要求而需要自托管沙箱基础设施的组织。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/24/top_stories_11242026'><span class='en'>Nov 24</span><span class='zh'>11月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>28</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50629936' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>5</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50354763" data-id="50354763" data-tags="Data|Programming|Code" data-score="27" data-comments="3" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50354763" data-time="1793725892">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ndrean/ZeBridge" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZeBridge – PostgreSQL replicas on devices, synchronized over NATS</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge：通过 NATS 同步设备上的 PostgreSQL 副本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS. It keeps a local database on each device and queues writes while the device is offline. When connectivity returns, PostgreSQL remains authoritative by applying rules, resolving conflicts, and returning a result to the application.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。它在每台设备上保留本地数据库，并在离线时排队保存写入操作。重新连接后，PostgreSQL 仍是权威数据源，负责应用规则、解决冲突并向应用返回结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>27</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50354763' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50355009" data-id="50355009" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="27" data-comments="11" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50355009" data-time="1793726721">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andrewboldi/tmux-restore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Tmux-restore saves tmux windows and agent sessions across reboots</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Tmux-restore 可在重启后保存 tmux 窗口和代理会话</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>tmux-restore restores tmux sessions, layouts, and scrollback after a reboot.</span><span class='zh'>tmux-restore 可在重启后恢复 tmux 会话、布局和滚动缓冲区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>tmux-restore restores tmux sessions, layouts, and scrollback after a reboot. It can also reopen conversations for Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, opencode, and pi. The project describes itself as reboot-proof tmux for coding agents.</span><span class='zh'>tmux-restore 可在重启后恢复 tmux 会话、布局和滚动缓冲区。它还能够重新打开 Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI、opencode 和 pi 中此前的对话。该项目将自己定位为面向编程代理的“重启无忧” tmux 工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>27</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50355009' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>11</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50602561" data-id="50602561" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="27" data-comments="4" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50602561" data-time="1795372857">
