<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/stass/type-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Type-C – variants, options, generics and pattern matching for C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Type-C——为 C 添加变体、选项、泛型和模式匹配</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Type-C is presented as a preprocessor and a full C11 superset that adds features such as variants, options, generics, and pattern matching while keeping the resulting code largely in C.</span><span class='zh'>Type-C 被设计为一个预处理器和完整的 C11 超集，在基本保持 C 代码形态的同时加入变体、选项、泛型和模式匹配等功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Type-C is presented as a preprocessor and a full C11 superset that adds features such as variants, options, generics, and pattern matching while keeping the resulting code largely in C. It is intended for parsing, tree walking, and complex error handling without requiring a complete rewrite, runtime library, or extra dependency. Files can use a .tc extension while the rest of a codebase remains unchanged, including kernel and bare-metal projects. The project includes examples and tests involving conversion of existing C code.</span><span class='zh'>Type-C 被设计为一个预处理器和完整的 C11 超集，在基本保持 C 代码形态的同时加入变体、选项、泛型和模式匹配等功能。它面向解析、树遍历和复杂错误处理场景，无需完整重写代码库，也不需要运行时库或额外依赖。用户可以将单个文件改为 .tc，代码库其余部分保持不变，内核和裸机项目也可使用。项目页面提供了示例，以及将现有 C 代码转换为 Type-C 的测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/4yk5/Ghostman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ghostman – A fast, keyboard-driven TUI API client and webhook inspector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Ghostman——快速、键盘驱动的 TUI API 客户端与 Webhook 检查器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal. It is local-first, requires no cloud account, uses no Electron, and is presented as a lightweight alternative to heavier modern API clients.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。它以本地优先为设计理念，无需云账户，也不使用 Electron，定位是更轻量的现代 API 客户端替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anteloc/ldraw-mecha/tree/v0.5.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agents infer Lego mechanisms from sources, then animate them</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：代理从资料推断 LEGO 机构并为其制作动画</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LDraw Mecha uses agentic language models to analyze annotated LDraw LEGO model files, infer their submodels and mechanisms, plan animations, and execute them in Blender.</span><span class='zh'>LDraw Mecha 使用代理式语言模型分析带注释的 LDraw LEGO 模型文件，推断其中的子模型和机械结构，规划动画，并在 Blender 中执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LDraw Mecha uses agentic language models to analyze annotated LDraw LEGO model files, infer their submodels and mechanisms, plan animations, and execute them in Blender. It outputs a rigged and animated Blender file from an initial .mpd source file. The project builds on LDraw Nova, an earlier tool for generating LEGO CAD models from LDraw source code.</span><span class='zh'>LDraw Mecha 使用代理式语言模型分析带注释的 LDraw LEGO 模型文件，推断其中的子模型和机械结构，规划动画，并在 Blender 中执行。它可以从初始的 .mpd 源文件生成带绑定和动画的 Blender 文件。该项目建立在此前用于从 LDraw 源代码生成 LEGO CAD 模型的 LDraw Nova 之上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sdalberto/ia-da-sobrevivencia/blob/main/README.en.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Offline survival assistant – local LLM and Kiwix, runs from a USB stick</span><span class='zh'>离线生存助手：本地 LLM 与 Kiwix，可从 U 盘运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This survival assistant runs fully offline on Windows, Linux, and macOS, including from a laptop or USB drive.</span><span class='zh'>这款生存助手可在 Windows、Linux 和 macOS 上完全离线运行，也可以从笔记本电脑或 U 盘启动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This survival assistant runs fully offline on Windows, Linux, and macOS, including from a laptop or USB drive. It provides sourced guides on water, fire, food, first aid, and other topics in Portuguese, English, and Spanish, alongside a local LLM using llama.cpp or Ollama. Kiwix libraries provide additional offline reference material.</span><span class='zh'>这款生存助手可在 Windows、Linux 和 macOS 上完全离线运行，也可以从笔记本电脑或 U 盘启动。它提供葡萄牙语、英语和西班牙语的水、火、食物、急救等主题资料，并通过 llama.cpp 或 Ollama 运行本地 LLM。Kiwix 资料库则提供额外的离线参考内容。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>26</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50380273' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>16</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50487155" data-id="50487155" data-tags="Programming|Show HN|Hardware|Code" data-score="25" data-comments="4" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487155" data-time="1794592422">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ThrudTheBarbarian/xc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Compile the same source for GPUs and CPUs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：为 GPU 和 CPU 编译同一份源代码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The xc language and xcc compiler turn ordinary loops into both multicore CPU code and GPU kernels, targeting formats including MSL, PTX, SPIR-V, and WGSL without requiring an SDK.</span><span class='zh'>xc 语言及其 xcc 编译器会将普通循环编译为多核 CPU 代码和 GPU 内核，支持 MSL、PTX、SPIR-V 和 WGSL 等格式，且无需安装 SDK。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The xc language and xcc compiler turn ordinary loops into both multicore CPU code and GPU kernels, targeting formats including MSL, PTX, SPIR-V, and WGSL without requiring an SDK. At runtime, programs benchmark each block on the machine and keep the faster placement. Version 1.0.1 supports Linux, macOS, and Windows on x86-64 and Arm64, and the compiler can also process plain C loops through Clang’s front end.</span><span class='zh'>xc 语言及其 xcc 编译器会将普通循环编译为多核 CPU 代码和 GPU 内核，支持 MSL、PTX、SPIR-V 和 WGSL 等格式，且无需安装 SDK。程序运行时会在当前机器上测试每个代码块，并选择更快的执行位置。1.0.1 版本支持 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 上的 x86-64 与 Arm64，编译器还可以通过 Clang 前端处理普通 C 循环。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/22/top_stories_11222026'><span class='en'>Nov 22</span><span class='zh'>11月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>25</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50487155' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>4</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50604540" data-id="50604540" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="23" data-comments="5" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50604540" data-time="1795385928">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Localcode is presented as an agentic coding tool that runs local models on a Mac.</span><span class='zh'>Localcode 是一款在 Mac 上运行本地模型的智能编码工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Localcode is presented as an agentic coding tool that runs local models on a Mac. The project is open source and can be upgraded with a uv pip install command.</span><span class='zh'>Localcode 是一款在 Mac 上运行本地模型的智能编码工具。该项目采用开源方式，并可通过一条 uv pip install 命令升级。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sshah03/recalc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Recalc – Spreadsheet where cells can be Claude Code or Codex terminals</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Recalc——单元格可以运行 Claude Code 或 Codex 的电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session. Sessions can communicate through grid cells, named cells, or an MCP server, and can be continued in a regular terminal. The tool currently runs locally on macOS, with Linux support described as less tested.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。不同会话可以通过网格单元格、命名单元格或 MCP 服务器互相通信，也可以在普通终端中继续运行。目前该工具可在 macOS 本地运行，Linux 支持则尚未充分测试。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>22</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413970' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>5</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50401844" data-id="50401844" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Hardware|Code" data-score="21" data-comments="7" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50401844" data-time="1794028591">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rkaehn/uniform" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: JavaScript web apps with WASM layout and GPU rendering in 280 KB</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 WASM 布局和 GPU 渲染构建 280 KB 的 JavaScript 网页应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB. Its creator aims to avoid the platform limits of native frameworks, the HTML/CSS constraints of browser-engine approaches, and the size or language requirements of other cross-platform frameworks. The design bundles the UI framework with the application so developers can update it independently of platform release cycles.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。作者希望避免原生框架的平台限制、基于浏览器引擎方案所继承的 HTML/CSS 约束，以及其他跨平台框架的体积或语言要求。该设计将 UI 框架直接与应用打包，使开发者能够独立于平台发布周期更新它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>21</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50401844' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>7</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50235449" data-id="50235449" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="21" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235449" data-time="1792958013">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-research/kauldron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kauldron: Modular, scalable library to train ML models</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron：可模块化、可扩展的 ML 模型训练库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kauldron is a modular, scalable library for training machine learning models, aimed at simplifying large-scale training and experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron 是一个模块化、可扩展的机器学习模型训练库，旨在简化大规模训练与实验流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kauldron is a modular, scalable library for training machine learning models, aimed at simplifying large-scale training and experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron 是一个模块化、可扩展的机器学习模型训练库，旨在简化大规模训练与实验流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTodoApp/OpenTodo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OpenTodo – a self-hosted, local-first task manager in one Go binary</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OpenTodo——单个 Go 二进制文件实现的自托管、本地优先任务管理器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTodo is a free, open-source task manager that can be self-hosted or hosted for users. It works on phones and computers, supports offline use and shared lists, and keeps users’ data under their control. The project is designed as a local-first alternative to subscription-based task applications.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTodo 是一款免费开源的任务管理器，用户可以自行部署或交由他人托管。它支持手机和电脑、离线使用以及与家人或团队共享列表，并让用户掌控自己的数据。该项目旨在提供一种本地优先的替代方案，减少对订阅制任务应用的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/biolend83/tubehaus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TubeHaus – an open-source YouTube client with no Google account</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TubeHaus——无需 Google 账户的开源 YouTube 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TubeHaus is a private, self-hosted YouTube client available as a Docker Compose web app and native apps for Android, Windows, macOS, Linux, and iPhone/iPad.</span><span class='zh'>TubeHaus 是一款私密、自托管的 YouTube 客户端，提供 Docker Compose 网页版，以及 Android、Windows、macOS、Linux 和 iPhone/iPad 原生应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TubeHaus is a private, self-hosted YouTube client available as a Docker Compose web app and native apps for Android, Windows, macOS, Linux, and iPhone/iPad. It requires no Google account and provides no tracking. The client preserves subscriptions and history on the user&#x27;s own server or device while retaining a YouTube-like layout and keyboard shortcuts.</span><span class='zh'>TubeHaus 是一款私密、自托管的 YouTube 客户端，提供 Docker Compose 网页版，以及 Android、Windows、macOS、Linux 和 iPhone/iPad 原生应用。它无需 Google 账户，也不进行跟踪。用户可以将订阅和历史记录保存在自己的服务器或设备上，同时保留类似 YouTube 的布局和键盘快捷键。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jakegarrett13-afk/UV-5RM-Radio-Mod" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UV-5RM-Radio-Mod</span><span class='zh'>UV-5RM 无线电改装</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This custom firmware for Baofeng UV-5RM radios adds applications including texting and spectrum tools.</span><span class='zh'>这套 Baofeng UV-5RM 自定义固件新增了短信和频谱工具等应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This custom firmware for Baofeng UV-5RM radios adds applications including texting and spectrum tools. Its texting feature supports short messages between two radios with delivery confirmation and requires no phone, internet connection, or additional hardware. CHIRP will no longer communicate with the modified device, so channels must be programmed with the included applications.</span><span class='zh'>这套 Baofeng UV-5RM 自定义固件新增了短信和频谱工具等应用。短信功能支持两台无线电之间发送短消息并确认送达，不需要手机、互联网或额外硬件。改装后设备将无法再与 CHIRP 通信，因此必须使用随附应用配置频道。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Neighborhood-Grid/flatpoints" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: 3D to 2D, point clouds flattening (geometry images)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：3D 转 2D，展平点云（几何图像）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds. It maps each 3D point from a point cloud sampled on an unknown surface to a 2D pixel, arranging the points into a square grid. The resulting flat representation can serve as an image-like indexing structure.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。它把从未知曲面采样得到的每个三维点映射为二维像素，并将这些点排列到方形网格中。生成的平面表示可以作为类似图像的索引结构。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>16</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50427153' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>5</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50623028" data-id="50623028" data-tags="Science|Programming|Code" data-score="15" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50623028" data-time="1795489042">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/pportilla/atlas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1910</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pportilla/atlas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A fast CAS with a focus on resolution of singularities</span><span class='zh'>专注于奇点消解的快速计算机代数系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Atlas is a computer algebra system that performs exact computations with polynomial rings over the rationals, studies varieties at rational points, and resolves hypersurface singularities.</span><span class='zh'>Atlas 是一个计算机代数系统，能够在有理数上的多项式环中进行精确计算，研究有理点处的代数簇，并消解超曲面奇点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Atlas is a computer algebra system that performs exact computations with polynomial rings over the rationals, studies varieties at rational points, and resolves hypersurface singularities. It can be used interactively, from scripts, on the command line, or over HTTP. Its Resolution Zoo workspace lets users browse computed examples and inspect resolutions.</span><span class='zh'>Atlas 是一个计算机代数系统，能够在有理数上的多项式环中进行精确计算，研究有理点处的代数簇，并消解超曲面奇点。它支持交互式使用、脚本、命令行和 HTTP。其 Resolution Zoo 工作区可用于浏览计算示例并检查消解结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/25/top_stories_11252026'><span class='en'>Nov 25</span><span class='zh'>11月25日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>15</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50623028' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50509980" data-id="50509980" data-tags="Security|Programming|Code" data-score="14" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509980" data-time="1794771779">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Ymsniper/vault-share" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1924</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ymsniper/vault-share" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A live service duress prototype</span><span class='zh'>实时服务型胁迫原型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vault-share is a self-hosted, invite-only encrypted file-sharing application built with Flask. Each file uses its own AES-256-GCM key, which is sealed separately for every authorized reader using HPKE, while uploads are signed and integrity-checked. The prototype also supports duress passwords that reveal separate cover vaults and can require a USB flash drive for the master key.</span><span class='zh'>Vault-share 是一个基于 Flask 的自托管、仅限邀请的加密文件共享应用。每个文件使用独立的 AES-256-GCM 密钥，并通过 HPKE 分别封装给每位获授权的读取者；上传内容还会进行签名和完整性校验。该原型还支持在胁迫密码下打开独立的伪装保险库，并可要求使用 USB 闪存盘保存主密钥。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>14</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50509980' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50406043" data-id="50406043" data-tags="Open Source|Gaming|Code" data-score="13" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406043" data-time="1794070703">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/trizoza/dles/blob/main/assets/daily-games/DLES.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The largest open-source DLE collection</span><span class='zh'>最大的开源 DLE 收藏集</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is an open-source collection of assets for daily games.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一个面向每日游戏的开源资源收藏集。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is an open-source collection of assets for daily games. The available information provides no further details about the collection.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一个面向每日游戏的开源资源收藏集。现有信息未提供该收藏集的更多细节。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/SafeScript/safescript" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1956</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SafeScript/safescript" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SafeScript, a safe version of JavaScript for executing untrusted code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：SafeScript——用于执行不可信代码的安全版 JavaScript</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments. It excludes features including eval, new, globals, and prototype access, then compiles to plain JavaScript and PHP. The compiler is about 48 KB minified, or roughly 12 KB gzipped, and uses .safe files intended to be locally verifiable.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。它排除了 eval、new、全局变量和原型访问等功能，并可编译为普通 JavaScript 和 PHP。编译器压缩后约 48 KB、gzip 后约 12 KB，使用 .safe 文件格式，并支持本地验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50417478' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50589235" data-id="50589235" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="12" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50589235" data-time="1795269188">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/natsu0529/agent-mcts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1969</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/natsu0529/agent-mcts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: agent-mcts – Monte Carlo tree search on top of Claude Code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：agent-mcts——在 Claude Code 之上运行蒙特卡洛树搜索</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>agent-mcts is an open-source MIT-licensed harness that applies Monte Carlo Tree Search to coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex CLI.</span><span class='zh'>agent-mcts 是一个采用 MIT 许可的开源工具，可将蒙特卡洛树搜索应用于 Claude Code 和 Codex CLI 等编程代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>agent-mcts is an open-source MIT-licensed harness that applies Monte Carlo Tree Search to coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex CLI. Instead of following one solution path, it explores multiple branches, backtracks from dead ends, and directs more budget toward promising paths. The project has been verified with Claude Code and lists support for other agents.</span><span class='zh'>agent-mcts 是一个采用 MIT 许可的开源工具，可将蒙特卡洛树搜索应用于 Claude Code 和 Codex CLI 等编程代理。它不再只沿着一条方案路径前进，而是探索多个分支、从死路回退，并将更多预算投入到有希望的路径上。该项目已通过 Claude Code 验证，并列出了对其他代理的支持情况。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/21/top_stories_11212026'><span class='en'>Nov 21</span><span class='zh'>11月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50589235' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50405688" data-id="50405688" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="10" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405688" data-time="1794067672">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1988</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/datastructures-js" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS – The JavaScript data structures project</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS：JavaScript 数据结构项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support. The project also provides an associated planning board.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。项目还提供了配套的规划看板。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2002</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WolveKafka – Consume Kafka with a plain HTTP endpoint</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：WolveKafka——通过纯 HTTP 接口消费 Kafka</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language. It manages consumer groups, batching, retries, offsets, and dead-letter queues. The project includes a quickstart, examples, deployment guidance, and comparison material.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。它负责管理消费者组、批处理、重试、偏移量和死信队列。项目还提供快速入门、示例、部署指南和对比资料。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/agda/agda/releases/tag/v2.9.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2007</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/agda/agda/releases/tag/v2.9.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agda v2.9.0</span><span class='zh'>Agda v2.9.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agda 2.9.0 adds an experimental `-j` option for type-checking modules in parallel in batch mode. The release also includes substantial performance improvements and drops support for GHC 8.8, 8.10, and 9.0. Agda is a dependently typed programming language and interactive theorem prover.</span><span class='zh'>Agda 2.9.0 在批处理模式下新增实验性的 `-j` 选项，可并行进行模块类型检查。该版本还带来了显著的性能改进，并停止支持 GHC 8.8、8.10 和 9.0。Agda 是一种依赖类型编程语言和交互式定理证明器。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/davide-troiani/moeshift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2008</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/davide-troiani/moeshift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>M4 Pro vs. M5 Pro (24GB): Qwen 3.8 Flash Next at 374 Tok/S Prefill with MoEShift</span><span class='zh'>M4 Pro 对比 M5 Pro（24GB）：Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 使用 MoEShift 达到 374 Tok/s 预填充速度</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models. On a Mac mini with an M4 Pro and 24 GB of unified memory, it runs the 125-billion-parameter Qwen 3.8 Flash Next using under 10 GB of peak process memory. The project reports roughly half the peak memory usage of Strata and 2.6× faster long-prompt processing.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。在配备 M4 Pro 和 24GB 统一内存的 Mac mini 上，它运行 1250 亿参数的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 时，进程峰值内存低于 10GB。项目称，与 Strata 相比，它的峰值内存占用约少一半，长提示处理速度快 2.6 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50432586' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50472560" data-id="50472560" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="9" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472560" data-time="1794510595">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ori-drs/drs-vpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1452842016/74145be6-6bc1-412d-b748-7023227c2aca' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2010</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ori-drs/drs-vpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DRS-VPT. Localize a photo in a 3D point cloud without an initial pose</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：DRS-VPT——无需初始位姿，在三维点云中定位照片</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics. The same model works across driving LiDAR scans, indoor iPhone photos, and structure-from-motion maps. The authors provide the code, model, weights, and an online demo.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。同一个模型可用于驾驶场景的 LiDAR 扫描、室内 iPhone 照片和运动恢复结构地图。作者提供了代码、模型、权重以及在线演示。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50472560' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50548763" data-id="50548763" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="9" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50548763" data-time="1795021213">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/voygr-tech/placebench" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2017</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/voygr-tech/placebench" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PlaceBench – Testing AI on opening and closing hours (and last trains)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：PlaceBench——测试 AI 对营业时间和末班车的判断</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PlaceBench benchmarks AI systems on place-based questions such as restaurant recommendations, opening hours, and last trains.</span><span class='zh'>PlaceBench 用餐厅推荐、营业时间和末班车等地点相关问题测试 AI 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PlaceBench benchmarks AI systems on place-based questions such as restaurant recommendations, opening hours, and last trains. The creators previously found that, even with web grounding, the best model fabricated or recommended closed places in one out of ten cases; personal assistants performed better but took longer. In the newer comparison, OpenAI&#x27;s Dots performed best and was also among the fastest, while xAI&#x27;s Grok Bot performed worst. Google Ask Maps was measured separately because it is not technically a personal assistant.</span><span class='zh'>PlaceBench 用餐厅推荐、营业时间和末班车等地点相关问题测试 AI 系统。创建者此前发现，即使使用联网检索，表现最好的模型仍有十分之一的推荐是虚构地点或已关闭地点；个人助手表现更好，但回答时间更长。在新一轮比较中，OpenAI 的 Dots 表现最佳，同时也是速度最快的系统之一；xAI 的 Grok Bot 表现最差。Google Ask Maps 被单独评估，因为它在技术上并非个人助手。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2021</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Viktotovich/vb-harness-for-agentic-building" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VB Harness – Make local coding agents verify their own work</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness——让本地编程智能体自行验证工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VB Harness makes local coding agents work in small tasks and isolated copies, using a project’s own checks to validate each change. It supports LM Studio, Ollama, and other OpenAI-compatible models, and retains changes only when type checks, linting, builds, tests, and independent review succeed. The tool is designed to make local agents accountable without trying to make the underlying model smarter.</span><span class='zh'>VB Harness 将本地编程智能体的工作拆分为小任务，并在隔离副本中执行，通过项目自身的检查来验证每次修改。它支持 LM Studio、Ollama 和其他兼容 OpenAI API 的模型，只有在类型检查、代码检查、构建、测试和独立复审均通过后才会保留改动。该工具旨在让本地智能体对结果负责，而不是试图直接提升底层模型的智能程度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50550829' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50474106" data-id="50474106" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="8" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106" data-time="1794516784">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Cerulion – ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime with multi-process replay</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Cerulion——支持 ROS 2、带多进程回放的机器人运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust. It aims to reduce inter-process communication latency without requiring a single process or restricting messages to plain-old-data types. Its multi-process graphs can be deterministically resimulated, and existing ROS 2 nodes and launch files can run alongside native Cerulion Rust or Python code.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。它旨在降低进程间通信延迟，同时不要求将程序合并为单进程，也不限制消息必须使用普通数据类型。其多进程图可以进行确定性重演，现有 ROS 2 节点和 launch 文件也能与原生 Cerulion Rust 或 Python 代码并行运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>8</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50635055" data-id="50635055" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="8" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50635055" data-time="1795556024">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion#harness-results" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion#harness-results" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Cerulion – Harness for Robotics, Gemini 3.8 Flash Matches GPT-6 Astra</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Cerulion——机器人开发框架，Gemini 3.8 Flash 达到 GPT-6 Astra 水平</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a robotics harness that gives language models tools to observe and move a robot while using measurements to close the control loop.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个机器人开发框架，为语言模型提供观察和移动机器人的工具，并通过测量结果形成闭环控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a robotics harness that gives language models tools to observe and move a robot while using measurements to close the control loop. On the LIBERO-PRO benchmark, Gemini 3.8 Flash with Cerulion solved 73 of 100 tasks, compared with 71 for GPT-6 Astra using a three-tool baseline. The authors report that Gemini’s baseline solved 25 tasks and was about 12 times cheaper and three times faster per run than GPT-6 Astra’s baseline.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个机器人开发框架，为语言模型提供观察和移动机器人的工具，并通过测量结果形成闭环控制。在 LIBERO-PRO 基准上，搭配 Cerulion 的 Gemini 3.8 Flash 完成了 100 项任务中的 73 项，而使用三工具基线的 GPT-6 Astra 完成了 71 项。作者称，Gemini 的基线只完成 25 项任务，而 Cerulion 方案每次运行的成本约低 12 倍、耗时约短 3 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/25/top_stories_11252026'><span class='en'>Nov 25</span><span class='zh'>11月25日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>8</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50635055' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50598297" data-id="50598297" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="7" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50598297" data-time="1795343945">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/b1tw153/overpass-ebnf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Overpass QL EBNF Grammar</span><span class='zh'>Overpass QL 的 EBNF 语法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project provides a formal grammar for the Overpass Query Language in W3C EBNF notation.</span><span class='zh'>该项目以 W3C EBNF 表示法提供 Overpass Query Language 的形式语法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project provides a formal grammar for the Overpass Query Language in W3C EBNF notation. The grammar can be loaded into a railroad-diagram generator for visual inspection or checked with the REx Parser Generator. It can also serve as input for generating a parser.</span><span class='zh'>该项目以 W3C EBNF 表示法提供 Overpass Query Language 的形式语法。用户可以将语法输入铁路图生成器进行可视化检查，或使用 REx Parser Generator 验证。它还可以作为生成解析器的输入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Getterbetter/ember/releases/tag/v1.0.1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Light control for MacBook charger status light (open-source)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：开源控制 MacBook 充电器状态灯</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project lets users control the status light on a MacBook charger. It supports turning the light on or off automatically based on time or Focus status, as well as keeping it persistently on or off. The project invites feature suggestions through GitHub.</span><span class='zh'>这个开源项目可以控制 MacBook 充电器的状态灯。它支持根据时间或专注模式状态自动开关灯，也可以让灯持续亮起或关闭。项目通过 GitHub 征集功能建议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A USB-C LTE modem that gives my computer a phone line</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一款通过 USB-C 为电脑提供电话线路的 LTE 调制解调器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C. With a nano-SIM and one USB-C cable, a computer can route call audio to a headset, hand calls to a script or AI agent, and send texts. The four-layer KiCad board was designed from a one-page specification and reviewed against component datasheets.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。插入 nano-SIM 并连接一根 USB-C 线后，电脑可以把通话音频发送到耳机，也可以将通话交给脚本或 AI 代理处理，并发送短信。这块四层 KiCad 电路板根据一页规格说明设计，并逐项对照元件数据手册进行审核。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>7</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317331' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50643304" data-id="50643304" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="6" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50643304" data-time="1795620530">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/spaces/dlouapre/looped-transformers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Looped Transformers: Thinking twice with the same weights</span><span class='zh'>循环 Transformer：用同一组权重思考两次</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This interactive page demonstrates looped transformers using small models.</span><span class='zh'>这个交互式页面使用小型模型演示循环 Transformer。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This interactive page demonstrates looped transformers using small models. The model repeatedly passes tokens through the same core layers with shared weights, displaying each pass and the final next-token prediction.</span><span class='zh'>这个交互式页面使用小型模型演示循环 Transformer。模型让 token 反复通过共享同一组权重的核心层，并展示每次循环以及最终的下一个 token 预测。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RenderBear/haci" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: My compact, bio inspired distributed edge detection algorithm</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的紧凑型仿生分布式边缘检测算法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex. Its current benchmark results fall between traditional methods such as Canny and Sobel and modern deep neural networks, while retaining the interpretability of the former. The author plans to improve video frame rates and performance across different hardware, and is seeking collaborators.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。目前的基准测试结果介于 Canny、Sobel 等传统方法与现代深度神经网络之间，同时保留了传统方法的可解释性。作者计划提升视频处理帧率和不同硬件上的性能，并寻找合作伙伴。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TanmayArya-1p/async-a-sync" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Implicit futures for C – the first access to a buffer is the await</span><span class='zh'>C 的隐式 future：首次访问缓冲区就是 await</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Async-a-sync is a compiler and runtime system built on Fil-C that lets ordinary blocking-looking C code run asynchronously underneath.</span><span class='zh'>Async-a-sync 是构建在 Fil-C 之上的编译器和运行时系统，让表面上阻塞的普通 C 代码在底层异步运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Async-a-sync is a compiler and runtime system built on Fil-C that lets ordinary blocking-looking C code run asynchronously underneath. It introduces implicit asynchronous futures without adding syntax, with the first access to a buffer acting as the await point.</span><span class='zh'>Async-a-sync 是构建在 Fil-C 之上的编译器和运行时系统，让表面上阻塞的普通 C 代码在底层异步运行。它无需新增语法即可引入隐式异步 future，并将首次访问缓冲区作为 await 点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OmniJev/Flide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Flide: Make Jev-like decision models up to 9.5x faster</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Flide，让 Jev 类决策模型最高提速 9.5 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models. The project claims up to a 9.5x speedup over vLLM and SGLang and currently supports more than 30 popular local Jev-like models. It can be installed with pip using the package name flide.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。项目称其速度最高可达 vLLM 和 SGLang 的 9.5 倍，目前支持 30 多种热门本地 Jev 类模型。用户可以通过 pip 安装名为 flide 的软件包。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmSach/maple" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Maple – Sub-2-BPW Moe Pruning to Run Gemma 4 26B at 45 TPS on a 6GB GPU</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Maple——次于 2 BPW 的 MoE 剪枝，让 Gemma 4 26B 在 6GB GPU 上达到 45 TPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models. It compresses Gemma 4 26B-A4B from 9.92 GB to 4.75 GB, equivalent to 1.48 effective bits per parameter. The project reports 100% in-VRAM execution on 6 GB laptop GPUs at 45.4 tokens per second.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。它将 Gemma 4 26B-A4B 从 9.92 GB 压缩到 4.75 GB，相当于每参数 1.48 个有效比特。项目报告称，该模型可在 6GB 笔记本 GPU 上完全载入显存运行，速度达到每秒 45.4 个 token。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50619725" data-id="50619725" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50619725" data-time="1795469839">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/frazik-main/.vibed" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Vibed – describe an app, then keep changing it</span><span class='zh'>Vibed：描述一个应用，然后持续修改它</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vibed is a blank desktop shell that becomes an application based on what the user describes.</span><span class='zh'>Vibed 是一个空白桌面外壳，会根据用户的描述变成相应的应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vibed is a blank desktop shell that becomes an application based on what the user describes. Users can describe later changes, and the application changes accordingly; it can then be changed again by the owner or by someone who receives it. The project treats an application as an evolving artifact rather than a finished object.</span><span class='zh'>Vibed 是一个空白桌面外壳，会根据用户的描述变成相应的应用。用户可以继续描述后续修改，应用便会随之改变；接收应用的人也可以再次修改它。该项目将应用视为持续演化的对象，而不是完成后不再变化的成品。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/23/top_stories_11232026'><span class='en'>Nov 23</span><span class='zh'>11月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50619725' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50554941" data-id="50554941" data-tags="DevOps|Programming|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554941" data-time="1795051843">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2133</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ri4dH/failtrace" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FailTrace – Reproduce a failed GitHub Actions run locally from its URL</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace：通过 URL 在本地复现失败的 GitHub Actions 运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FailTrace takes the URL of a failed GitHub Actions run and identifies the failed job and step. It matches that failure to the workflow file at the exact commit, extracts the relevant log output, and explains what is needed to reproduce the issue locally. The tool is designed to reduce guesswork when debugging CI failures.</span><span class='zh'>FailTrace 接收失败的 GitHub Actions 运行 URL，并找出失败的作业和步骤。它会将失败过程与对应提交中的工作流文件匹配，提取相关日志，并说明在本地复现问题所需的条件。该工具旨在减少调试 CI 失败时的猜测。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/18/top_stories_11182026'><span class='en'>Nov 18</span><span class='zh'>11月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50554941' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50511761" data-id="50511761" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50511761" data-time="1794783217">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/itsmeyaw/skillset" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2138</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/itsmeyaw/skillset" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Skillset: Easily curate and install agent skills collection</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Skillset，轻松整理并安装代理技能集合</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Skillset groups programming, backend, and other agent skills into collections that can be installed with one command from a JSON file.</span><span class='zh'>Skillset 将编程、后端等代理技能整理成集合，用户可以通过一个 JSON 文件和一条命令安装所需集合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Skillset groups programming, backend, and other agent skills into collections that can be installed with one command from a JSON file. It wraps the Vercel Skill CLI and uses it as the underlying library, aiming to preserve compatibility with the standard CLI across teams. The project is designed to avoid installing unnecessary skills globally and consuming excess context.</span><span class='zh'>Skillset 将编程、后端等代理技能整理成集合，用户可以通过一个 JSON 文件和一条命令安装所需集合。它封装并直接使用 Vercel Skill CLI，旨在保持与标准 CLI 的团队间兼容性。该项目希望避免全局安装不必要的技能并消耗过多上下文。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Radio-in-a-box: Self-hosted internet radio with Icecast, Liquidsoap and Rust CLI</span><span class='zh'>Radio-in-a-box：使用 Icecast、Liquidsoap 和 Rust CLI 的自托管网络广播箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio. The project emphasizes portability, reproducibility, and a minimal maintenance footprint for hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。强调可移植性、可重复性与低维护成本，面向爱好者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awake-client/awake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Git friendly open API client</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections. The project began as a side project and became an everyday tool for its creators. It is presented as a Git-friendly open API client.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。该项目最初是一个业余项目，后来成为创建者日常使用的工具。它被定位为一款对 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端。</span></p>
